坦纳着起急来。眼看要分秒必争了。他把表攥在手心，数着里程碑一块块甩在身后。这会儿已四点半，还有九英里要赶。

===Original===
At last they came to the town. But here matters instead of mending, grew much worse. Neither Tanner nor the driver knew the streets, and precious minutes were wasted trying to puzzle out the way from the rather inferior map the latter had brought.

===Chinese===
他们终于进了城。可在这儿，事情非但不曾好转，反而更糟了。坦纳和司机都不认得街路，司机带的那张地图又不济事，为琢磨怎么走，白白耗掉了宝贵的分分秒秒。

===Original===
Quarter to five. Tanner was in desperation. And then to his relief his eye fell on a policeman. It was the work of a moment to call him over, explain the situation, and get him up beside the driver. Then their troubles were over. The streets were empty and they made fine speed.

===Chinese===
五点差一刻。坦纳绝望了。恰在此时，他一眼瞥见一名警察，顿时如释重负。把警察唤过来、说明情形、请他坐上司机旁边，前后不过一眨眼的工夫。这下难题解了。街上空荡荡的，车跑得飞快。

===Original===
It wanted ten minutes to five as the car pulled up at the docks, and Tanner leaped out and raced to the berth of the great liner. A man whom he instantly recognised as a policeman in plain clothes stood near the bottom of each gangway, while a third was sauntering along the edge of the wharf beside the boat. Tanner spoke hurriedly to the latter.

===Chinese===
汽车停靠码头时，离五点还差十分钟。坦纳跳下车，直奔大班轮的泊位。两条舷梯脚下各站着一个坦纳一眼认出是便衣警察的人，第三个人正沿船边的码头沿踱来踱去。坦纳疾步找上第三位。

===Original===
“He’s not on board, sir,” the man answered. “We were here before he could have got down from the Town Station, and besides we made inquiries.”

===Chinese===
「他不在船上，先生，」那人答道。「他最早也得从城站那边下来，我们赶在了他前头，况且我们还查问过。」

===Original===
“The other side of the ship?” queried the Inspector.

===Chinese===
「船那一侧呢？」探长问。

===Original===
“We have a man rowing up and down.”

===Chinese===
「我们有一条小艇来回巡着。」

===Original===
Tanner grunted.

===Chinese===
坦纳哼了一声。

===Original===
“Who’s in charge?” he asked.

===Chinese===
「这儿谁负责？」他问。

===Original===
“Sergeant Holmes. He went to phone the station. He’ll be back directly.”

===Chinese===
「霍尔姆斯（Holmes）警佐。他去所里打电话了，马上回来。」

===Original===
Tanner was woefully disappointed. He felt that if Douglas was not already aboard he would never risk it now. Had the man, he wondered, been sharper than he had counted on, and once again given him the slip? Fortunately, he had taken the obvious precaution of wiring all the stations at which the 7:32 stopped, so that, even if Douglas had alighted elsewhere, he would almost certainly be spotted. But had Douglas travelled by the 7:32 at all? Was his haste with the taxi and his purchase of the ticket another trick, and was he lying low in Exeter, intending still further to alter his appearance and make a bolt elsewhere? Or was he walking all night with the object of joining a train at some quite different station in the morning? Tanner could not guess.

===Chinese===
坦纳失望到了极点。他心想，倘若道格拉斯还没上船，此刻就决不敢再冒险上来了。莫非那人比他估计的更精明，又给他来了个金蝉脱壳？所幸他把想到的防范都做了，给 7:32 沿线停靠的各站都拍了电报，这样即使道格拉斯在别处下了车，也几乎必定会被人认出来。可是，道格拉斯到底坐没坐那班 7:32？雇出租汽车的火急、买票的匆忙，会不会又是一个幌子，他其实正猫在埃克塞特，打算进一步改头换面，另寻路线脱身？又或者，他正连夜步行，想赶明早在一个完全不同的车站上车？坦纳猜不透。

===Original===
Three minutes only remained and Tanner grew more and more anxious. It was now or never. Then, as the gangways were being hoisted, a sergeant of police appeared and went up to one of the plain clothes men. Tanner hurried forward.

===Chinese===
只剩三分钟，坦纳越来越焦灼。成败在此一举。正在舷梯吊起之际，一名警佐出现，朝其中一个便衣走去。坦纳抢步迎上。

===Original===
“Mr. Tanner, sir?” said the sergeant. “I’m very sorry, sir, but you’re late.”

===Chinese===
「是坦纳先生吗，先生？」警佐说。「非常抱歉，先生，您晚了一步。」

===Original===
“Late?” Tanner cried sharply. “What do you mean, sergeant? There’s plenty of time to go on board still.”

===Chinese===
「晚了？」坦纳厉声问。「什么意思，警佐？现在上船还来得及得很。」

===Original===
The sergeant shook his head.

===Chinese===
警佐摇了摇头。

===Original===
“He’s not there, sir. He’s gone. I’ve just learnt that he left by the *Vaal River*. She sailed at four o’clock.”

===Chinese===
「他不在船上，先生。人已经走了。我刚获悉，他是坐「瓦尔河」（Vaal River）号走的。船四点钟就开了。」

===Original===
“Damnation!” cried Tanner angrily. “What were you thinking about, sergeant? How in hell did you let him slip through your fingers?”

===Chinese===
「该死！」坦纳怒喝。「你们脑子里在想什么，警佐？究竟是怎么搞的，竟让他从你们指缝里溜掉了？」

===Original===
“The man I sent down, sir, missed him. I can’t imagine how he did it, but you’ll hear what he has to say yourself. After I had all﻿—”

===Chinese===
「我派下去的人把他漏了，先生。我想不出他怎么会漏掉，不过他自己怎么交代，您马上可以亲耳听。等我把所有——」

===Original===
“I’ll see him,” said Tanner grimly. “How did you find it out?”

===Chinese===
「我会找他问话的，」坦纳冷冷地说。「这消息你是怎么得来的？」

===Original===
“I posted the men, sir, first, then I went round them myself. I got to the *Vaal River*’s berth as she was sheering out. I made inquiries at the office. There is no doubt the man booked.”

===Chinese===
「我先把弟兄们布出去，先生，然后自己挨处查巡。赶到「瓦尔河」号的泊位时，那条船正拐头驶出。我到营业处查问过了，那人确实买了票，没有疑问。」

===Original===
“Where to?”

===Chinese===
「去哪儿？」

===Original===
“Tangier, sir.”

===Chinese===
「丹吉尔（Tangier），先生。」

===Original===
“H’m﻿—Morocco, and there’s no extradition from there. Where else does the boat call?”

===Chinese===
「唔——摩洛哥，那儿引渡不了。这船沿途还在哪儿停靠？」

===Original===
“Lisbon, Marseilles, Naples, Suez, Delagoa Bay and Durban.”

===Chinese===
「里斯本（Lisbon）、马赛、那不勒斯、苏伊士、德拉瓜湾（Delagoa Bay）和德班（Durban）。」

===Original===
“I’ll get him at Lisbon. Show me the office.”

===Chinese===
「我在里斯本截住他。带我去营业处。」

===Original===
They hurried down to the East Africa Line Quay office. There Tanner interviewed the booking clerk and satisfied himself that Douglas really had sailed. He had booked under the name of Walter Donnell.

===Chinese===
两人疾步赶往东非轮船公司（East Africa Line）的码头营业处。坦纳盘问了售票员，确证道格拉斯确实上了船——他登记用的名字是沃尔特·唐奈尔（Walter Donnell）。

===Original===
“Lisbon is the first call?” asked the Inspector.

===Chinese===
「里斯本是第一停靠港吧？」探长问。

===Original===
“Yes. She’s due there about six on Thursday morning.”

===Chinese===
「是。定于星期四早上六点前后到港。」

===Original===
“And this is Tuesday. That’s about a fifty hour run?”

===Chinese===
「今天是星期二。这一程约莫五十个钟头？」

===Original===
“About that.”

===Chinese===
「差不多。」

===Original===
“I must get there before her. How am I to do it?”

===Chinese===
「我必须赶在它前头到。怎么个走法？」

===Original===
The clerk stared.

===Chinese===
售票员瞪大了眼。

===Original===
“I’m afraid you can’t,” he answered slowly. “She’s not a specially fast boat, but there’s no other leaving soon enough to pass her.”

===Chinese===
「恐怕办不到，」他慢吞吞地答。「她不算特别快的船，可是再没有一条船开得够早，能赶到她前头。」

===Original===
“Overland?”

===Chinese===
「走陆路呢？」

===Original===
“No. There’s not time. If you had caught the Havre boat last night you could have done it. You can’t now.”

===Chinese===
「不行。时间来不及。您昨晚要是赶上了去阿弗尔的船，还办得到。现在不行了。」

===Original===
“Let me see the time table.”

===Chinese===
「把时刻表给我瞧瞧。」

===Original===
The clerk produced a *Continental Bradshaw*.

===Chinese===
售票员取出一本《欧陆布拉德肖》（Continental Bradshaw）。

===Original===
“Here you are,” he said, turning to the “Through Routes” on page 6. “You see there are two trains a day from Paris to Lisbon. One, the ordinary, leaves Paris at 10:22 at night. It gets to Lisbon at 12:33 two nights later﻿—that is, about a fifty hours’ run. That’s out of the question, and you’ll see the other is too. It’s a special fast train, the Sud Express, and it leaves Paris at 12:17 midday, and reaches Lisbon at 10:50 the following evening﻿—that is thirty-four hours and a half. Now if you could catch that train today you’d be all right. But you couldn’t. Even if you could catch the 8:00 a.m. from Victoria, which you couldn’t. That would only bring you into Paris at 5:29﻿—five hours late.”

===Chinese===
「喏，」他说着翻到第 6 页的「直达路线」。「您看，每天有两班车从巴黎去里斯本。一班是普通车，夜里 10:22 离巴黎，隔两夜 12:33 才到里斯本——也就是路上要走大约五十个钟头。这班免谈；另一班您看，也一样。那是趟特别快车，「南方快车」（Sud Express），中午 12:17 离巴黎，次日晚 10:50 到里斯本——三十四个半钟头。您今天要是能赶上那班车就成了。可惜赶不上。哪怕能赶上维多利亚（Victoria）站 8:00 的早班车——事实上也赶不上——到巴黎也是 5:29，晚了五个钟头。」

===Original===
“How long does your boat lie at Lisbon?”

===Chinese===
「你们的船在里斯本停多久？」

===Original===
“About four hours. She’s due away about ten on Thursday morning.”

===Chinese===
「大约四个钟头。定于星期四上午十点前后离港。」

===Original===
Tanner felt he was up against it. So far as he could see it was impossible for him to reach Lisbon before 10:50 on the Thursday night, and by that time the man he wanted would already have left some twelve hours. And if he missed him at Lisbon, he would miss him for good. He could never get him once he was ashore at Tangier. Nor was it any more possible for another officer from the Yard to go in his place.

===Chinese===
坦纳觉得自己撞上了死结。依他看，无论如何也赶不到星期四晚 10:50 之前的里斯本，而到那时，他要抓的人早已走了约莫十二个钟头。在里斯本一旦错过，就是永远错过。那人只要在丹吉尔上了岸，就再也抓他不着。派苏格兰场别的警探替他去，也同样办不到。

===Original===
Of course, there were the Portuguese police. Tanner had never been in Portugal, and knew nothing whatever about its police, but he had the not uncommon insular distrust of foreign efficiency. As he put it to himself, he would rather rely on himself any day than trust to any of these foreign chaps. But there seemed no other way.

===Chinese===
当然，还有葡萄牙警察可以指望。坦纳从未到过葡萄牙，对那里的警察一无所知，可他怀着英国岛民常见的那份成见，不大瞧得上外国人的办事效率。用他自己的话说，这帮外国佬靠不靠得住，他信不过；哪一天都宁愿靠他自己。不过眼下似乎又没有别的路可走。

===Original===
Absently thanking the clerk, he walked with the sergeant back to his car and drove to the police station. As he dismounted an idea shot suddenly into his mind.

===Chinese===
他心不在焉地道了声谢，同警佐走回汽车，开到警察所。下车之际，一个念头蓦地闪进脑海。

===Original===
“Get the car ready for another run,” he shouted and hurrying to the telephone, put through a call to Scotland Yard.

===Chinese===
「把车备好，还要跑一趟！」他喊道，随即奔向电话，接通了苏格兰场。

===Original===
“Yes, I’m Tanner,” he said, when the connection was made. “The Ponson Case. That man Douglas I’m after got away on the *Vaal River*. Sailed from Southampton at four this morning. First call Lisbon. I must be there to meet him. It can only be done if I leave Paris at 12:17 today. None of the ordinary services would get me over in time. Can you arrange with the Air people to give me a plane?”

===Chinese===
「对，我是坦纳，」接通后他说。「庞森案。我追的那个道格拉斯坐「瓦尔河」号跑了。今晨四点从南安普敦起航，第一停靠港里斯本。我必须赶到那儿截他。只有今天 12:17 离开巴黎才办得到。寻常的班轮都来不及把我送过海。能不能同航空方面说妥，给我一架飞机？」

===Original===
He was told to wait, and at six o’clock the reply came.

===Chinese===
对方让他等着。六点钟，回电来了。

===Original===
“The Deputy Chief has arranged for a fast plane to leave the drome near Petersfield as soon as possible. Get there at once and report to Major Forbes. Call at Hendon and we shall have French and Portuguese money for you, as well as the extradition warrant.”

===Chinese===
「副总监已安排一架快机，尽快自彼得斯菲尔德（Petersfield）附近的机场起飞。即刻赶到那里，向福布斯（Forbes）少校报到。中途在亨登（Hendon）落一下，我们备好法国和葡萄牙的钞票，还有引渡令，交给您。」

===Original===
Tanner was not long in reaching Petersfield, but there was a delay at the aerodrome, and he chafed impatiently as the precious minutes slipped away. It was not indeed till a little after seven that the actual start was made. The morning was clear at first, and they made good speed to Hendon, alighting and picking up the money and papers. But as they reached the coast they ran into a haze, which soon developed into a thick fog. The pilot did his best, going straight on by dead reckoning, but when in another hour they got through it, they found they had gone a good deal out of their course in a northerly direction. Tanner swore bitterly, for he found his margin of time was growing less and less. Finally they picked up the main line of the Northern Railway, and following it fairly closely, at last saw creeping up over the horizon the buildings of the capital.

===Chinese===
坦纳不多时便赶到了彼得斯菲尔德，可在机场又出了耽搁，宝贵的分分秒秒从指间溜走，急得他直跳脚。实实拖到七点过一点儿，飞机才当真起飞。清晨起初天朗气清，他们顺利飞抵亨登，降落取了钱和文书。可一到海岸便扎进一片薄霭，转眼酿成浓雾。飞行员竭尽所能，凭航位推算一路直飞，可再过一个钟头钻出雾团时，发现自己已向北偏离航线老远。坦纳狠狠咒骂，眼见时间的富余越缩越小。最后他们认出了北方铁路的干线，循着它紧随而行，终于望见都城的建筑群从地平线上缓缓浮起。

===Original===
“Down in ten minutes,” the pilot roared, and Tanner nodded as he looked his watch.

===Chinese===
「十分钟后落地！」飞行员吼道。坦纳看了看表，点头。

===Original===
It was eleven minutes to twelve, and the Inspector recognised he would have to run for it. Soon they were above Argenteuil and crossing the great loops of the Seine, with St. Cloud on the right and the vast city stretching away to the left. Now they were planing rapidly down, till with a gentle shock they alighted at the edge of the flying ground at Issy. Tanner leaped out and ran to the entrance as fast as the stiffness of his legs would allow. As he did so twelve sounded from the clock towers. He had seventeen minutes, the Gare Quai d’Orsay was two miles away, and there were no taxis within sight.

===Chinese===
十二点差十一分，探长明白接下来只有撒腿跑了。转眼他们已飞临阿让特伊（Argenteuil）上空，横穿塞纳河的大河曲，右边是圣克卢（St. Cloud），左边，茫茫都城铺展而去。飞机急速下滑，轻轻一震，落在伊西（Issy）飞行场的边上。坦纳跳出来，凭两条坐僵的腿拼命向场口奔去。就在这时，各处钟楼敲响了十二点。他还有十七分钟，奥赛河岸车站（Gare Quai d'Orsay）远在两英里外，而视野之内一辆出租汽车也没有。

===Original===
There was but one thing to do and Tanner did it. Some private cars were drawn up on the road just outside the flying ground. Tanner ran his eye hastily over them and selected one, a racing car from which a sporting looking man was just descending. The detective hailed him.

===Chinese===
摆在眼前的只有一条路，坦纳立刻走了。飞行场外的路上正停着几辆私人汽车。坦纳飞快扫了一眼，挑中一辆赛车，一位运动家风度的男士正从车上下来。探长迎上去招呼。

===Original===
“Sir,” he panted, “I have crossed by aeroplane from England to catch the 12:17 at the Gare Quai d’Orsay, and now I can’t get a taxi. If you would run me till we meet a taxi, I just couldn’t say how grateful I’d be.”

===Chinese===
「先生，」他气喘吁吁地说，「我坐飞机从英国赶来，要赶奥赛河岸车站 12:17 的车，这下却雇不到出租车。您要是肯捎我一段，捎到碰上出租车为止，您不知道我会有多感激。」

===Original===
The man looked puzzled.

===Chinese===
那人一脸茫然。

===Original===
“I not speak Engleesh,” he said slowly, then adding interrogatively, “You weesh﻿—*aller, aller*﻿—go﻿—*à la* Gare Quai d’Orsay?”

===Chinese===
「我，不会讲英语，」他慢吞吞地说，随即疑问地补上一句：「您是想——*aller，aller*——去——*à la*——奥赛河岸车站？」

===Original===
Tanner nodded emphatically, and taking out his watch, ran his finger from the minute hand, which was now standing at five minutes past the hour, to seventeen minutes past. The man threw up his left hand to signify comprehension.

===Chinese===
坦纳重重点头，掏出表，手指从分针——这时正指过五分——一路划到过十七分。那人左手一扬，表示心领神会。

===Original===
“*Ah, oui*,” he answered. “*Bon. Montez vite, monsieur.* Chomp een.”

===Chinese===
「*Ah, oui*（啊，对），」他答道。「*Bon. Montez vite, monsieur.*（好。快上车吧，先生。）乔姆普·因——快上车！」

===Original===
Tanner had obeyed the gesture before the man finished speaking, and the powerful car, swinging round, shot rapidly eastwards along the *quais*.

===Chinese===
那人话音未落，坦纳已应着手势钻进车里。大功率的汽车一个甩身，沿着河滨大道（*quais*）疾速向东射去。

===Original===
“Where you﻿—*allez*﻿—go?” jerked out the man as they tore along. Tanner understood.

===Chinese===
「您——*allez*——去——哪儿？」车飞驰中那人一字一顿地问。坦纳听懂了。

===Original===
“Lisbon,” he called.

===Chinese===
「里斯本。」他喊道。

===Original===
“Ah, Lisbonne. *Oui*,” the man nodded.

===Chinese===
「啊，里斯本。*Oui*（对）。」那人点头。

===Original===
Suddenly they came to a great building﻿—Tanner did not know his Paris﻿—and the car stopped abruptly. The man jumped out followed by his passenger. As they ran into the concourse of the huge Quai d’Orsay Station, the hands of the clock pointed to fifteen minutes past twelve. Two minutes to get the ticket! Without his new friend Tanner would have been utterly lost. The taking of a ticket seemed a complicated and interminable affair. But at last it was accomplished, and Tanner raced for the bridge across the low level tracks. But just before he reached the inclined plane descending to the platform, the ticket examiner slammed the gate. There was a voluble outcry from the sporting man, but for answer the official shrugged his shoulders and pointed to the roofs of the carriages. The train was already moving.

===Chinese===
猛地，他们来到一座巨大的建筑前——坦纳对巴黎两眼一抹黑——汽车戛然停住。那人跳下车，乘客紧随其后。两人奔进奥赛河岸车站巨大的站厅时，大钟的针正指十二点十五分。两分钟买票！没有这位新朋友，坦纳准得抓瞎。买票这桩事仿佛繁缛得没完没了，可总算办成了，坦纳撒腿奔向横跨低线轨道的天桥。可就在快到下站台的斜坡时，检票员砰地关上了栅栏门。那位运动家连珠炮似的抗议了一通，检票员只耸耸肩，指了指车厢顶——火车已经动了。

===Original===
Once again Tanner swore bitterly, as he gazed at the disappearing vehicles. But his friend gave him no time for self-commiseration.

===Chinese===
望着渐渐远去的车厢，坦纳又一次狠狠咒骂。可他的朋友没给他自伤自怜的工夫。

===Original===
“*Vite! Vite!*” he cried, signing to the other to follow him, and rushing once more out of the station.

===Chinese===
「*Vite! Vite!*（快！快！）」他喊道，招手叫坦纳跟上，再度冲出车站。

===Original===
They threw themselves into the car, which started off at a furious pace eastwards. Then Tanner recollected that the terminus of the Paris-Orleans line had formerly been the Gare d’Austerlitz farther up the river, the Gare Quai d’Orsay being a new station at the end of a recently made extension. All trains, he farther remembered having read, stopped at the Gare d’Austerlitz to enable the electric engine which worked through the extension tunnel to be replaced by a steam locomotive. Evidently his friend thought he could overtake the train at the Austerlitz Station.

===Chinese===
两人扑进汽车，车向东狂驰而去。这时坦纳才想起：巴黎—奥尔良线的终点站，原先是河更上游的奥斯特里茨车站（Gare d'Austerlitz），奥赛河岸车站是新筑延长线端头的新站。他还记得在哪儿读到过，所有列车都要在奥斯特里茨停靠，好把牵引延长线隧道的电力机车换下，挂上蒸汽机车。看来他的朋友打的算盘，正是在奥斯特里茨站追上这班车。

===Original===
And he did﻿—just. After wringing the hand of the man who had taken so much trouble to help him, he dashed to the platform and climbed into a carriage as the train began to move.

===Chinese===
他也真追上了——就差那么一线。坦纳紧握了这位不辞辛劳相助的先生的手，冲上站台，在车起步的一刻攀进车厢。

===Original===
“Lord!” he said to himself as he wiped his forehead, “only for that old sport I’d have missed it.”

===Chinese===
「老天！」他抹着额头自语。「若不是这位老兄，可就误了大事。」

===Original===
Then began a long tedious journey. Though the train was rapid and luxurious, Tanner was pretty sick of it before he reached his destination. There was a restaurant car forward, and as they raced across the sunny country south of Paris, the Inspector did full justice to an excellent lunch.

===Chinese===
接着开始了一段漫长而乏味的旅程。火车又快又豪华，可还没到目的地，坦纳已经腻烦透了。前部挂着餐车，列车飞掠过巴黎以南阳光灿烂的原野时，探长美美地享用了一顿极佳的午餐。

===Original===
After a time he grew wearied by the monotony of the flat lands, but the scenery became more interesting as they crossed the hills between Poitiers and Angoulême. Bordeaux was passed about seven o’clock, and as darkness fell they were traversing the dreary, desolate, sandy wastes and pine forests of Les Landes.

===Chinese===
过了一阵，平坦单调的原野叫他看得倦了，可等火车翻过普瓦捷（Poitiers）与昂古莱姆（Angoulême）之间的丘陵，景致又渐渐有趣起来。七点前后过了波尔多（Bordeaux），夜幕降临时，列车正穿行在朗德（Les Landes）荒凉萧索的沙地和松林之间。

===Original===
They reached Irun just before midnight, changing there into the broad-gauge carriages of Spain, and waiting for customs examination.

===Chinese===
午夜前夕抵达伊伦（Irun），在那里换上西班牙的宽轨车厢，等候海关查验。

===Original===
The moon rose as they passed through the rocky country north of Burgos, and it was daylight when they reached the latter town. Then on again through Valladolid to Medina, where the Madrid portion of the train branched off; through Salamanca of legendary fame, but now, for Spain, a considerable railway centre, then into Portugal, where the train hurtled along at considerably over thirty miles an hour. Finally, with brakes grinding, they descended the steep incline tunnelled beneath one of the seven hills on which Lisbon is built, and pulled up, twenty minutes late, in the Rocio Station.

===Chinese===
列车穿过布尔戈斯（Burgos）以北的岩石山地时，月亮升了起来；抵达布尔戈斯时，天已破晓。再往前，经巴利亚多利德（Valladolid）到梅迪纳（Medina），去马德里的那部分车厢在那里摘走；又经过传说满天、如今在西班牙也算得上大铁路枢纽的萨拉曼卡（Salamanca），随后进入葡萄牙，火车以明显超过每小时三十英里的速度疾驰。最后，闸瓦磨得吱嘎作响，列车滑下隧道贯穿的陡坡——那是里斯本所倚七山之一——晚点二十分钟，停进了罗西奥（Rocio）车站。

===Original===
When Tanner emerged into the brilliantly lighted streets and gazed down the splendid vista of the Avenida da Liberdade, he literally held his breath with amazement. The Portuguese he had always looked on as a lazy, good-for-nothing set, but this great new boulevard made him reconsider his opinion. He booked a room in the Avenida Palace Hotel, and then, crossing the Dom Pedro Square, walked down to the steamboat offices in the Rua da Alfandega.

===Chinese===
坦纳走到灯火通明的街上，沿自由大道（Avenida da Liberdade）壮丽的远景放眼望去，着实惊得屏住了呼吸。他一向把葡萄牙人看作一群懒惰的废物，可这条崭新的宏伟大道让他不得不重新掂量自己的成见。他在阿维尼达宫旅馆（Avenida Palace Hotel）订了个房间，然后穿过唐佩德罗广场（Dom Pedro Square），步行前往阿尔凡德加街（Rua da Alfandega）上的轮船营业处。

===Original===
The office was open﻿—everyone seemed to be on the move all night﻿—and one of the clerks spoke English. The steamer, it appeared, was due about half-past six. Tanner took the clerk into his confidence, and the latter made arrangements for the Inspector to get aboard with the first boat from the shore.

===Chinese===
营业处开着门——这里的人仿佛彻夜都在走动——职员中有一位会讲英语。轮船定于六点半前后到港。坦纳向那职员交了底，职员便作安排，让探长随第一班离岸的小艇登轮。

===Original===
At six o’clock Tanner was down on the Praça do Commercio, admiring in the brilliant sunlight the splendid river which flowed before him, and the charming setting of the town on its range of hills. In the river lay several steamers, some quite large, and all tugging at their anchors with their bows upstream. Down seawards, but inside the comparatively narrow mouth of the Tagus, a grey, two-funnelled boat was coming slowly up﻿—the *Vaal River*﻿—with, as Tanner hoped, William Douglas on board.

===Chinese===
六点钟，坦纳已站在商业广场（Praça do Commercio）上，在灿烂的晨光里欣赏着眼前流淌的大河，和这座依着层层山冈铺展、风姿动人的城市。河中停着几艘轮船，有几条相当大，全都船头朝上游，拽着锚链微微摆动。往下游方向、塔古斯河（Tagus）相当狭窄的河口内侧，一艘灰色双烟囱的船正缓缓驶来——正是「瓦尔河」号，而且，如坦纳所愿，威廉·道格拉斯就在船上。

===Original===
His friend the clerk arriving a moment later, the two men embarked on a motor launch. As the *Vaal River*’s anchor fell with a mighty splash, they sheered alongside and made fast.

===Chinese===
他的朋友那位职员随后赶到，两人登上一艘摩托艇。「瓦尔河」号的铁锚轰然砸出巨响水花时，他们已贴着船舷靠上，系住了缆。

===Original===
When the port authorities had gone aboard, Tanner was allowed to follow. He went straight to the captain, who was still on the bridge, and showing him his card, explained his business.

===Chinese===
等港口官员们登船之后，坦纳获准跟进。他径直去见还在舰桥上的船长，出示名片，说明了来意。

===Original===
“And so Mr. Walter Donnell’s wanted for murder,” the captain commented. “Guess he’s aboard all right. I thought he had something on his mind. See the chief steward and you’ll find him. What are you going to do with him?”

===Chinese===
「这么说，沃尔特·唐奈尔先生是因命案受通缉咯，」船长说。「我看他八成在船上。我早觉得他心里有事。去找事务长（chief steward），准能找到。您打算怎么处置他？」

===Original===
“Take him back to London.”

===Chinese===
「押回伦敦。」

===Original===
“Of course. But how?”

===Chinese===
「那当然。可怎么押？」

===Original===
“I don’t know. What would you advise?”

===Chinese===
「我也没数。您有何高见？」

===Original===
The captain pointed to a single-funnelled steamer of about 4,000 tons lying not two hundred yards on their port quarter.

===Chinese===
船长指了指停在他们左舷后方不到二百码处的一艘单烟囱轮船，约莫 4,000 吨。

===Original===
“That’s the *Chrysostom*, a Booth liner, due out in about an hour. If you take my advice you’ll get aboard and don’t favour the shore with your presence. I’ll run you over in the launch.”

===Chinese===
「那是「克里索斯托」（Chrysostom）号，布思轮船公司（Booth）的船，个把钟头后启航。听我一句劝，您干脆上那条船待着，别劳大驾再在岸上露面。我这就用摩托艇送您过去。」

===Original===
Tanner thanked the man warmly.

===Chinese===
坦纳热诚地道了谢。

===Original===
“Guess that’s all right,” he answered dryly. “I’m as interested in getting him out of my ship as you are in taking him.”

===Chinese===
「哪儿的话，」他干巴巴地答。「把他从我这船上弄走，您有多热心，我就有多热心。」

===Original===
Finding the chief steward, Tanner explained the matter in hand, adding that he wished to make the arrest as quietly as possible. The man seemed mildly interested and promised his help.

===Chinese===
坦纳找到事务长，说明手头的事，并补充说，他希望逮捕办得越安静越好。那人似有几分兴致，一口答应帮忙。

===Original===
Douglas, alias Donnell, was, it appeared, still in his cabin, and the two went thither. He was in bed, and rose to open the door. When he saw Tanner his eyes started from his head with amazement, then his jaw dropped and his face went grey. Stepping quickly back, he collapsed on to the cabin sofa and sat staring helplessly.

===Chinese===
道格拉斯——化名唐奈尔——据说还在舱里，两人便寻了去。他还没起床，起身开了门。一看见坦纳，他惊得两眼发直，随即下巴松了，脸灰了。他急步后退，瘫坐在舱房沙发上，呆呆望着，全无办法。

===Original===
“William Douglas or Walter Donnell,” Tanner said solemnly, “I arrest you on a charge of being concerned in the death of the late Sir William Ponson, of Luce Manor, Halford. I have to warn you that anything you say will be used against you.”

===Chinese===
「威廉·道格拉斯也罢，沃尔特·唐奈尔也罢，」坦纳郑重地说，「我以涉嫌谋害已故哈尔福德卢斯庄园威廉·庞森爵士的罪名逮捕你。我得警告你，你说的任何话，将来都可能用作不利于你的证据。」

===Original===
The man made a desperate effort to pull himself together.

===Chinese===
那人拼命稳住自己。

===Original===
“My God!” he gasped. “How did you trace me?” Then, Tanner not replying, he went on with pitiable earnestness:

===Chinese===
「我的天！」他喘着气。「你们是怎么追到我头上的？」坦纳不答，他便又可怜巴巴地、急切切地说下去：

===Original===
“But you’ve made a mistake. I am innocent. I know the circumstances look bad, but I’m innocent, I swear it in God’s name.”

===Chinese===
「可你们弄错了。我是清白的。我知道情形看着糟，可我清白，我拿上帝的名义起誓。」

===Original===
“That will do,” said Tanner not unkindly. “You’ll have every chance to put yourself right if you can do it. But you’ll have to come back to London with me. And for your own sake, the less you say the better.”

===Chinese===
「够了，」坦纳说，语气并不严苛。「只要办得到，你有的是机会把事情说清。可你得跟我回伦敦。为你自己着想，还是少说为妙。」

===Original===
For a moment the idea of making a desperate resistance seemed to cross the prisoner’s mind. Then, apparently realising his hopeless position, he said quietly, “I’ll go with you. Let me pack my things.”

===Chinese===
一时间，犯人眼里似乎掠过拼死一搏的念头。随即，他大约认清了处境无望，平静地说：「我跟您走。让我收拾收拾东西。」

===Original===
Tanner nodded, keeping a keen eye on the other’s movements for fear he would attempt suicide. But such an idea did not seem to occur to him. He dressed and packed expeditiously enough, and then said he was ready to go.

===Chinese===
坦纳点头应允，眼睛紧盯对方一举一动，惟恐他寻短见。可那人似乎压根儿没起这念头。他穿衣收拾，手脚倒还麻利，随即说可以走了。

===Original===
The launch was waiting, and in a few minutes they stood on the deck of the *Chrysostom*, homeward bound. Presently the anchor was hoisted and the vessel, swinging round, commenced her 1,200 mile trip to Liverpool.

===Chinese===
摩托艇正等着，几分钟后，两人已站在踏上归程的「克里索斯托」号甲板上。不多时锚起，船身一转，开始了驶往利物浦（Liverpool）的 1,200 英里航程。

===Original===
Having explained his business to the captain and seen Douglas securely locked in a cabin, Tanner stood leaning on the rail of the upper deck, watching the pleasantly situated town slip slowly astern. He could see the Cathedral of Belem standing, damaged, just as it was left by the earthquake of 1755. Then out of the mouth of the river and past the picturesque pleasure resort of Mont Estoril, with, just beyond it, the sleepy, old-world village of Cascaes till, rolling easily in the Atlantic swell, they turned northwards. The Burlings Islands, which they passed later in the day, were the last land they saw until, on the third morning, they awoke to find themselves lying in the Mersey. By midday Tanner and his prisoner were in London.

===Chinese===
向船长说明来意、看着道格拉斯被稳稳妥妥锁进一间舱房之后，坦纳倚着上甲板的栏杆，目送这座坐落有致的城市缓缓退向船尾。他望得见贝伦大教堂（Cathedral of Belem），带着残损，仍保持着 1755 年大地震后遗留的模样。随后，船驶出河口，掠过风光如画的避暑胜地埃斯托里尔山（Mont Estoril），再往前，是那座困倦的古旧村落卡斯凯什（Cascaes）；接着，船在大西洋的长涌中从容起伏，转向北去。当天晚些时候驶过的伯林群岛（Burlings Islands），是他们见到的最后一片陆地。直到第三天早晨，他们醒来时，船已泊在默西河（Mersey）中。当天中午，坦纳和他的囚犯到了伦敦。

## XIII · Blackmail? / XIII · 敲诈？

===Original===
When Inspector Tanner reached his office in New Scotland Yard, he found an instruction from Chief Inspector Edgar, informing him that Mr. James Daunt, of Lincoln’s Inn, had important evidence to give him relative to the Ponson case. Accordingly, after he had made a formal report on his Portuguese expedition, he called up Jimmy and arranged a meeting. A few hours later he was seated in the solicitor’s office, smoking one of the latter’s best cigars.

===Chinese===
坦纳探长回到新苏格兰场自己的办公室，发现埃德加总探长留有一条指示，告知他林肯律师学院的詹姆斯·多恩特先生有关于庞森案的重要证据要向他提供。于是，他就这趟葡萄牙之行做了正式汇报之后，给吉米挂了个电话，约定会面。几个小时后，他已坐进这位律师的办公室，抽上了对方顶好的雪茄。

===Original===
“My Chief says you have something to tell me?” he began, after mutual greetings.

===Chinese===
寒暄过后，他先开了口：「你们头儿说，你有事要告诉我？」

===Original===
“Why yes,” Jimmy replied. “Did your Chief tell you what it was?”

===Chinese===
「是啊，」吉米答道，「你们头儿跟你说是什么事了吗？」

===Original===
“Didn’t see him. He’s in Manchester.”

===Chinese===
「没见到他本人。他去曼彻斯特（Manchester）了。」

===Original===
“I fancy you’ll be surprised. You recollect you told me you had suspected Cosgrove Ponson, but that he had established an alibi and so must be innocent?”

===Chinese===
「我想你会大吃一惊。你还记得吧，你曾告诉过我，你怀疑过科斯格罗夫·庞森，但他立住了一桩不在场证明，所以必定是清白的？」

===Original===
Tanner nodded as he drew at his cigar.

===Chinese===
坦纳吸了口雪茄，点了点头。

===Original===
“That’s right,” he agreed.

===Chinese===
「不错。」他表示同意。

===Original===
“You were satisfied the alibi was sound?”

===Chinese===
「你当时认定那不在场证明无懈可击？」

===Original===
“Absolutely.”

===Chinese===
「完全肯定。」

===Original===
“It’s a fake,” said Jimmy quietly.

===Chinese===
「那是假的。」吉米不动声色地说。

===Original===
Tanner took his cigar out of his mouth and looked at the other.

===Chinese===
坦纳从嘴里取下雪茄，望着对方。

===Original===
“Get along now, Mr. Daunt,” he answered. “You’re trying to pull my leg.”

===Chinese===
「得了吧，多恩特先生，」他答道，「你这是拿我寻开心。」

===Original===
“No. The thing’s a fake right enough. Cosgrove was at the boathouse that night.”

===Chinese===
「不是寻开心。那东西确实是假的。案发那晚，科斯格罗夫就在船屋。」

===Original===
Tanner stared incredulously.

===Chinese===
坦纳瞪着他，满脸不敢相信。

===Original===
“You seem in earnest,” he said slowly. “But you’ve made a mistake. I went into it carefully. There’s no doubt it’s sound.”

===Chinese===
「你像是当真的，」他缓缓说道，「可你弄错了。那件事我仔细查证过，毫无疑问站得住脚。」

===Original===
“It’s *you* that have made the mistake,” Daunt answered pleasantly, and he went on to tell the Inspector what he and Lois had done, and all they had discovered.

===Chinese===
「弄错的是*你*。」多恩特和颜悦色地答道，接着便向探长讲起他和洛伊丝两人做了些什么，以及他们的全部发现。

===Original===
To say that Tanner was amazed and disappointed would be to understate the case. He was woefully chagrined.

===Chinese===
说坦纳又惊又失望，还嫌说轻了。他懊恼得无以复加。

===Original===
“God bless my soul!” he cried, “but that sort of takes a chap down. Here was I looking down on you and that splendid girl as a pair of meddling nuisances, and I’m blowed if you haven’t had it over on me all the time.”

===Chinese===
「我的老天爷！」他叫道，「这可真叫人下不来台。我一直把你们俩——你，还有那位了不起的姑娘——当作一对多管闲事的讨厌鬼，打心眼儿里瞧不上，岂料你们从头到尾都占了我的上风，这可真是活见鬼。」

===Original===
“Well,” said Jimmy, “tit for tat.”

===Chinese===
「这个嘛，」吉米说，「一报还一报。」

===Original===
The Inspector eyed him almost aggressively.

===Chinese===
探长几乎带着挑衅打量他。

===Original===
“And what now?” he demanded.

===Chinese===
「那么，下一步呢？」他质问道。

===Original===
“Why this. I’ve told you what we did about Cosgrove. Now you tell me what took you to Portugal.”

===Chinese===
「是这样：关于科斯格罗夫，我们做的事我已经告诉你了。现在该你告诉我，是什么把你引到葡萄牙去的。」

===Original===
“Oh, that,” answered Tanner looking relieved. “It’s irregular, but I’m blessed if I care.” He re-lit his cigar, which in his agitation he had allowed to go out, and beginning with the day of the adjourned inquest, he recounted his adventures in London and in Devon, the midnight run to Southampton, the flight to Paris, the journey to Lisbon, and finally the arrest of William Douglas. When he had finished, James Daunt was nearly as surprised and mystified as the Inspector had been a few minutes earlier.

===Chinese===
「哦，那个啊，」坦纳答道，神色松了下来，「这事是不合规矩，可我豁出去了，管不了那许多。」他重新点上雪茄——方才一阵激动，雪茄已经让他弄灭了——便从验尸审讯休会那天讲起，把这之后的历险一一道来：在伦敦，在德文郡，午夜驱车南安普敦，飞奔巴黎，奔赴里斯本，直到最后逮捕威廉·道格拉斯。他讲完时，詹姆斯·多恩特的惊愕和困惑，几乎不亚于几分钟前的探长。

===Original===
“ ’Pon my soul, a most extraordinary business,” he commented. “There’s Austin, first suspected, then cleared, then suspected again and arrested, and now cleared again. Then there’s Cosgrove, first suspected, then cleared, and now suspected again. And now, here’s a third man mixed up in the thing. I suppose the next thing that comes out will clear Douglas!”

===Chinese===
「我的老天，这真是一桩离奇至极的案子。」他议论道，「先说奥斯汀，起先受嫌疑，随后洗清，接着又受嫌疑、遭逮捕，如今又洗清了。再说科斯格罗夫，起先受嫌疑，随后洗清，如今又受了嫌疑。而现在，又有一个第三者搅了进来。我看下一步冒出来的，就该替道格拉斯洗冤了！」

===Original===
“I don’t think,” Tanner answered. “But what do you mean by saying Austin is now cleared again? It’s the first I’ve heard of that.”

===Chinese===
「我看不见得。」坦纳答道，「不过，你说奥斯汀如今又洗清了，这是什么意思？这我还是头一回听说。」

===Original===
“Why, Cosgrove was clearly impersonating him.”

===Chinese===
「嘿，科斯格罗夫分明是在假扮他呀。」

===Original===
“Not on your life,” said Tanner with decision. “Mark my words, Mr. Daunt, they were all there﻿—Sir William and Austin and Cosgrove and Douglas. Every blooming one of them was there. See here,” he continued as the other showed signs of dissent, “there’s evidence against every one of them. Sir William was seen there. Austin was seen too, and there’s no doubt he faked that business about the shoes. Yes, I know,” as Daunt would have spoken, “his story about the shoes seems all right, and it’s very clever, but it won’t wash. He was seen there, and he was there. Then Cosgrove was there, for you’ve proved that. And lastly this man Douglas was there also, for I saw his footmarks on the boathouse floor. Yes, they were all there, and there’s some conspiracy between them.”

===Chinese===
「绝无可能。」坦纳斩钉截铁地说，「记住我的话，多恩特先生：他们那天晚上全都在场——威廉爵士、奥斯汀、科斯格罗夫，还有道格拉斯。一个不落，个个都在。你听着，」见对方露出不服的神色，他接着说道，「他们每一个人头上都压着不利于他的证据。有人看见威廉爵士到过那儿。奥斯汀也被人看见了，而且毫无疑问，鞋子那出把戏是他一手伪造的。是的，我知道，」见多恩特想要开口，他说，「他那套关于鞋子的说法听着像那么回事，也确实巧妙，可是站不住脚。有人看见他在那儿，那么他就是在那儿。其次，科斯格罗夫也在那儿，这一点是你们证实了的。最后，这个道格拉斯同样在场，因为我在船屋地板上看见了他的脚印。是的，他们全都在场，他们彼此之间有一场密谋。」

===Original===
Though Daunt had to admit this conclusion seemed sound, it was by no means what he wished the Inspector to arrive at. His business was to clear Austin, and while the bringing in of first Cosgrove and now this man Douglas had at the time seemed all to the good, it did not help if it merely led to a conspiracy charge. But Tanner’s voice broke into his cogitations.

===Chinese===
多恩特不得不承认，这个结论听着站得住脚，可它绝不是他希望探长得出的结果。他的职责是为奥斯汀洗冤；先前扯进一个科斯格罗夫、如今又扯进这个道格拉斯，当时看来全是好事，但倘若到头来只落得一条同谋罪，那就于事无补了。不料坦纳的声音打断了他的沉思。

===Original===
“You see,” the detective said, following on his own line of thought, “they were together in London. Sir William, Austin, and Cosgrove lunched together﻿—*both* Austin and Cosgrove denying it, mind you﻿—and then immediately Sir William went to see Douglas. There was some business between the four of them. There’s not a doubt of it.”

===Chinese===
「你要知道，」这位侦探顺着自己的思路说下去，「他们当时都在伦敦。威廉爵士、奥斯汀和科斯格罗夫是一起吃的午饭——记住，奥斯汀和科斯格罗夫*两个都*矢口否认——饭后威廉爵士立刻就去见道格拉斯。他们四人之间有桩交易。这一点毫无疑问。」

===Original===
It gave Daunt a nasty shock to recall that Austin had to him also denied having seen Sir William on that Monday. If it could be proved that Austin had lied about this, as apparently it could, what reliance could be placed on any of his other statements?

===Chinese===
多恩特想起一件事，心里好不吃惊：那个星期一见过威廉爵士一事，奥斯汀对他也是矢口否认的。倘若这一点可以证明奥斯汀撒了谎——看样子确实证明得了——那他其余的供述，还能让人相信几分？

===Original===
There was silence for some moments, and then Daunt moved impatiently.

===Chinese===
两人默然片刻，随后多恩特焦躁地挪了挪身子。

===Original===
“Well, what are you going to do about it?” he asked.

===Chinese===
「那好，这件事你打算怎么办？」他问。

===Original===
“Get the names of that taxi-man and those other witnesses from you,” the Inspector answered promptly, “and check over your conclusions about Cosgrove. Not that I doubt you, of course, but it’s business. Then if I’m satisfied, I’ll arrest him. Among his or Douglas’s papers there’ll be surely something to put us on the track.”

===Chinese===
「先从你这儿拿到那个出租车司机和其余证人的姓名，」探长应声答道，「再把你们关于科斯格罗夫的结论核实一遍。倒不是信不过你，这是公事程序。之后我若觉得没问题，就逮捕他。在他或者道格拉斯的文件里，总会有什么东西能给我们指点门径。」

===Original===
When Tanner had taken his leave Daunt sat motionless for some minutes, thinking over what he had just heard. And the more he thought, the less he liked the turn affairs had taken. All his doubts as to Austin’s innocence had returned. If his client had really met Sir William on the Monday in question, why had he denied it? It would take an even more ingenious explanation to account for it than that he had given about the shoes.

===Chinese===
坦纳告辞之后，多恩特一动不动坐了好几分钟，细细回味方才听到的话。他越想越觉得事态的走向不妙。他对奥斯汀清白与否的种种疑心，又都回来了。倘若他的委托人那个星期一真的见过威廉爵士，那他为什么要否认？要解释这一层，恐怕得拿出比他那套鞋子的说辞更高明得多的解释才行。

===Original===
To satisfy himself, when his work for the day was finished, Daunt put a photograph of Austin in his pocket and drove to the Étoile restaurant in Soho. But a few moments’ inquiry was sufficient to convince him. Austin had been there beyond question, and therefore his statement to Daunt had been a direct falsehood.

===Chinese===
为了弄个明白，当天事务办完之后，多恩特揣上一张奥斯汀的照片，驱车前往索霍区的「埃托瓦尔」餐馆。只问了几句，他便信了。奥斯汀确实到过那里，毫无可疑；如此说来，他对多恩特所说的话便是彻头彻尾的谎话。

===Original===
Sorely puzzled as to what he should say to Lois, Jimmy Daunt returned to his rooms. There after much thought he decided he would see Austin next morning and tax him directly with the lie.

===Chinese===
吉米·多恩特回到寓所，苦于不知该对洛伊丝如何开口。苦苦思量之后，他决定第二天上午去见奥斯汀，就那句谎话当面诘问他。

===Original===
Another point had been worrying him. He recalled his surprise at the manner in which Austin had received the news of his and Lois’s discovery that Cosgrove had been at the boathouse on the fatal night. Austin had professed incredulity, but all the same had seemed terribly shocked. He had ridiculed their idea that Cosgrove could have been impersonating him, and utterly refused to sanction a defence on these lines.

===Chinese===
另有一桩事也一直让他放心不下。他记得，当他跟洛伊丝把那个发现——案发当晚科斯格罗夫到过船屋——告诉奥斯汀时，后者那副神气令他好生诧异。奥斯汀口称难以置信，可看上去仍是震惊万分。他把两人那个「科斯格罗夫可能是在假扮奥斯汀」的想法奚落了一通，并且断然拒绝认可照这个思路去辩护。

===Original===
At the time Daunt had put this down to cousinly affection, but in the light of Tanner’s theories it seemed to take on a more sinister interpretation. What if Tanner were right, and both cousins were involved in the murder? Would not that make a horribly complete explanation of Austin’s attitude? Might the latter not fear that the bringing in of Cosgrove might be a step towards the elucidation of the whole affair? It was therefore with foreboding that Daunt set out next morning to see his client.

===Chinese===
当时多恩特把这一切归之于表兄弟的情分，可如今照坦纳的理论看来，这里面似乎另有一层凶险的解释。万一坦纳说对了呢？万一这对堂兄弟双双卷进了谋杀呢？那样一来，奥斯汀的态度岂不是得到了一个可怕而圆满的解释？他会不会正是害怕把科斯格罗夫扯进来，会成为揭开整个案情的第一个台阶？因此第二天上午动身去见委托人时，多恩特心头怀着不祥的预感。

===Original===
He had determined to try a little test. He conversed at first as on previous visits, and then when the other’s mind was occupied and he was off his guard, he said suddenly, but as carelessly as he could, “By the way, William Douglas has been arrested.”

===Chinese===
他已决意试一个小小的考验。起初他照旧寒暄闲谈，同前几次探望一样；待到对方心思被别的事占住、不设防备的时候，他突然而又尽量随意地说道：「对了，威廉·道格拉斯已经抓起来了。」

===Original===
The effect on Austin surpassed his most gloomy prognostications. Surprised out of himself, the accused man started back, his face paled and he gave vent to an exclamation of what seemed to Daunt to be veritable consternation. Then rapidly controlling himself, he tried to simulate indifference.

===Chinese===
这一下对奥斯汀的冲击，比他最悲观的预料还要强烈。被告猛地往后一缩，完全失了常态，脸色发白，发出一声在多恩特听来是真正惊惶失措的惊呼。随即他迅速克制住自己，装出一副淡漠的样子。

===Original===
“William Douglas?” he repeated questioningly, “I have heard my father speak of him. An old gardener, wasn’t he? What on earth has he been doing?”

===Chinese===
「威廉·道格拉斯？」他疑问似的重复了一遍，「我听我父亲说起过他。是个老园丁，对吧？他到底干了什么？」

===Original===
Daunt felt instinctively the reply did not ring true.

===Chinese===
多恩特本能地觉得这答话听着不真。

===Original===
“That’s what I’ve come to ask you,” he retorted. “What were you and he doing at the boathouse on that Wednesday night?”

===Chinese===
「这正是我来要问你的，」他反唇相诘，「那个星期三晚上，你同他两个人在船屋里做什么？」

===Original===
“My dear fellow,” Austin answered﻿—he was evidently shaken, but still spoke with a certain dignity﻿—“you forget yourself. You have no right to ask me such a question.”

===Chinese===
「我亲爱的朋友，」奥斯汀答道——他显然方寸已乱，但说话仍带着几分尊严——「你失态了。你没有权利问我这样的问题。」

===Original===
“Then I withdraw it and ask you another. You told me, I think, that the Sunday evening when you dined at Luce Manor was the last occasion on which you saw Sir William alive?”

===Chinese===
「那我收回这个问题，另问一个。我想你告诉过我，你在卢斯庄园吃晚饭的那个星期天晚上，是你最后一次见到活着的威廉爵士？」

===Original===
“Certainly.”

===Chinese===
「当然。」

===Original===
“And you repeat that now?”

===Chinese===
「这话你现在还作数？」

===Original===
“Why, of course I do.”

===Chinese===
「那还用说，当然作数。」

===Original===
Daunt leant forward and spoke impressively.

===Chinese===
多恩特俯身向前，语气郑重地说：

===Original===
“Then how do you explain your having lunched with him on the next day at the Étoile in Soho?”

===Chinese===
「那么，第二天你在索霍区的『埃托瓦尔』同他共进午餐，又作何解释？」

===Original===
Again Austin started. Daunt was sure that the shot had told. But the other only said:

===Chinese===
奥斯汀又是一惊。多恩格确信这一箭射中了要害。但对方只说：

===Original===
“It seems to me you have mistaken the side you’re on. Are you taking prosecuting counsel’s place?”

===Chinese===
「依我看，你把自己站的立场弄错了。你是要改行做控方律师吗？」

===Original===
“Good Lord, Ponson, don’t play with words,” cried the solicitor angrily. “It’s far too serious. If I’m to act for you, I must have an explanation of these things. Why have you denied being there when you were?”

===Chinese===
「天哪，庞森，别在字眼上兜圈子了，」律师怒气冲冲地喊道，「事情太严重了。我若要继续替你办案，这些事你必须给我一个解释。你明明在场，为什么矢口否认？」

===Original===
“Who says I was?”

===Chinese===
「谁说我在场？」

===Original===
“Everyone concerned. The manager, two waiters, the porter﻿—all agree. There’s no mistake. I saw them myself. Tanner knows all about your lunch there with Sir William and Cosgrove, and about Sir William’s visit afterwards to Douglas.”

===Chinese===
「凡是相关的人都说。经理、两名侍者、还有门童——众口一词。错不了，是我亲眼一一见过的。你在那儿同威廉爵士和科斯格罗夫吃午饭，饭后威廉爵士又去拜访道格拉斯，这些坦纳全知道了。」

===Original===
Austin was pale, and a look of positive dread showed for a moment in his eyes. But he preserved his calmness and only replied:

===Chinese===
奥斯汀脸色发白，眼里一时掠过一抹真真切切的恐惧。但他强自保持镇定，只答道：

===Original===
“They were mistaken. I was not there.”

===Chinese===
「他们认错人了。我不在那儿。」

===Original===
Daunt dropped his detached air, and spoke with all the earnestness at his command.

===Chinese===
多恩特收起了那副置身事外的神气，拿出全部诚恳说道：

===Original===
“Look here, Ponson,” he said. “What the truth in this wretched business is I don’t know, but I do know that for you to go on like this means a certain verdict of ‘Guilty.’ That’s as sure as you’re sitting there. If you don’t care about yourself, for God’s sake think of that girl that’s giving up her all for you. You must tell her the truth﻿—in common honour you must tell her. Your actions must look suspicious to her as well as others. If you can explain them, for Heaven’s sake do so, and if not, don’t let her commit herself too far to get out.”

===Chinese===
「你听着，庞森，」他说，「这桩倒霉案子的真相究竟如何，我不知道；可我知道，你再这样下去，等着的必然是一个『有罪』的裁决。这一点，跟你此刻坐在这里一样确凿。就算你不顾惜自己，看在老天分上，也替那位为你舍弃一切的姑娘想一想。你必须把真相告诉她——凭做人的本分，你也必须告诉她。你的种种行径，在她眼里必然也同在旁人眼里一样可疑。你若解释得清楚，千万快解释；若解释不清，就别让她越陷越深，脱不了身。」

===Original===
Austin slowly raised his head and smiled unhappily.

===Chinese===
奥斯汀缓缓抬起头，凄然一笑。

===Original===
“You’re a good fellow, Daunt,” he said. “God knows I’m ten times more anxious for Lois than for myself. But all I can tell you is to repeat what I have already said; I was not there. There must be some ghastly mistake.”

===Chinese===
「你是个好人，多恩特，」他说，「老天知道，我为洛伊丝焦心，十倍于为我自己。可我能告诉你的，只有把说过的话再说一遍：我不在那儿。这里面一定是出了可怕的误会。」

===Original===
Daunt felt his anger rising.

===Chinese===
多恩特觉得火气往上冒。

===Original===
“It’s a mistake that will cost you your life if you don’t rectify it,” he answered sharply. “If you can’t be open with me I must give up the case.”

===Chinese===
「这个误会你不扳过来，是会要你命的。」他尖刻地答道，「你若不能对我开诚布公，我就只好撒手不管这个案子了。」

===Original===
“Then you don’t believe me?”

===Chinese===
「这么说，你是不信我了？」

===Original===
“Believe you? How can I believe you? I show your photograph to four separate men at that café and all identify you without hesitation. But see here”﻿—he spoke as if a new idea had occurred to him﻿—“the thing can be easily settled. If you weren’t at the café with Sir William where were you? Tell me that?”

===Chinese===
「信你？叫我怎么信你？我把你的照片拿给那家餐馆里四个不同的人看，个个都毫不迟疑地认出了你。不过你听着，」他说得像是忽然想出了一个新主意，「这事轻易就能弄清楚。你既然没跟威廉爵士在那家餐馆，那你在哪儿？你说说看？」

===Original===
“I lunched that day at the Savoy.”

===Chinese===
「那天我是在萨伏伊吃的午饭。」

===Original===
“For three hours?”

===Chinese===
「一顿饭吃三个钟头？」

===Original===
“Well, no. I sat and smoked in the lounge. Then I got one or two things﻿—tobacco and those two pairs of shoes.”

===Chinese===
「唔，那倒没有。饭后我在休息厅里坐着抽了会儿烟。随后买了一两样东西——烟丝，还有那两双鞋。」

===Original===
Jimmy Daunt did not believe him, but all the persuasion of which he was a master failed to induce Austin, whatever he might or might not know, to supplement or vary his statement. But the latter consistently scouted the idea that the trial could end in a conviction, stoutly maintaining that there was no evidence to lead to such a conclusion.

===Chinese===
吉米·多恩特并不信他的话，但任凭他使出浑身的劝说功夫，也没能说动奥斯汀——不管他知情与否——把供述增补一字或改动一句。倒是后者始终嗤笑审判会定罪的说法，一口咬定没有任何证据能导出那样的结论。

===Original===
At last Jimmy took his leave, intensely dissatisfied with the result of the interview. As had been arranged between them, he sent a wire to Lois asking her to come to town that afternoon, though he looked forward to the meeting with anything but pleasure.

===Chinese===
最后吉米告辞而去，对这次会面的结果失望透顶。依照两人先前的约定，他给洛伊丝发了封电报，请她当天下午进城来，尽管他盼望这次会面，全无半点高兴可言。

===Original===
It was nearly five when she arrived. He greeted her with no hint that his news was bad, and as before insisted on an immediate visit to the quiet restaurant. Over a cup of tea he told her all of Tanner’s adventures and discoveries, with the single exception of his learning of the meeting between Sir William, Austin, and Cosgrove at the Étoile restaurant. But when they had returned to his office, he became more serious.

===Chinese===
她到时已近五点。他迎着她，丝毫没有露出消息不妙的意思，并且像上次一样，坚持立刻去那家清静的餐馆。斟上一杯茶，他把坦纳的种种历险和发现原原本本讲给她听，唯独瞒下了他查出威廉爵士、奥斯汀和科斯格罗夫在「埃托瓦尔」餐馆会面一事。可等两人回到他的办公室，他的神色凝重起来。

===Original===
“I’m frightfully sorry, Lois,” he began, after seeing that she was comfortably seated, “but I haven’t told you all the news yet, and I’m afraid the rest of it is not too good.”

===Chinese===
「洛伊丝，我非常抱歉，」等她安顿坐好，他开口道，「消息我还没有讲完，剩下那些，恐怕不大好。」

===Original===
Her expressive face became clouded and anxious, but she did not speak. Then Daunt told her as gently as he could of the lunch at the Étoile, and Tanner’s theories resulting therefrom.

===Chinese===
她那张富于表情的脸蒙上了阴云，露出忧色，但她没有作声。于是多恩特尽可能婉转地把「埃托瓦尔」那顿午饭，以及坦纳据此生出的种种推断，讲给了她。

===Original===
“But that’s not such bad news,” she said with evident relief. “Inspector Tanner must have made a mistake. Austin said he didn’t see his father after the Sunday evening.”

===Chinese===
「这算不上什么坏消息呀。」她说，明显松了口气，「一定是坦纳探长弄错了。奥斯汀说过，星期天晚上以后他没再见过他父亲。」

===Original===
Daunt moved uneasily. It was a confoundedly awkward job, and he wished he was through with it.

===Chinese===
多恩特坐立不安。这差事别扭透了，他恨不得早点了结。

===Original===
“Dear Lois, it sounds a perfectly horrible thing to say, but that is just the difficulty. In spite of Austin’s denial, Tanner is convinced the meeting took place. I believed he was mistaken, so I went down to the restaurant myself. I took Austin’s photograph, and the manager, two waiters, and the porter recognised it instantly. All four are prepared to swear Austin was there.”

===Chinese===
「亲爱的洛伊丝，这话听起来恐怕不近人情，可难就难在这里。尽管奥斯汀否认，坦纳却认定有过那次会面。我原以为他弄错了，便亲自去了那家餐馆。我带着奥斯汀的照片，经理、两名侍者，还有那个门童，一眼就认了出来。四个人都愿意宣誓作证，说奥斯汀到过那儿。」

===Original===
“Did you tell Austin?”

===Chinese===
「你跟奥斯汀说了吗？」

===Original===
“Yes. He stuck to his denial.”

===Chinese===
「说了。他还是一口咬定没去过。」

===Original===
Daunt had expected and feared an outbreak from Lois on hearing the news, but though her face showed extreme pain, she spoke very quietly.

===Chinese===
多恩特原以为洛伊丝听到这个消息会发作，也正怕她发作，可她虽然脸上露出极大的痛苦，说话却极平静。

===Original===
“There is no reason to suppose the four men in the café are dishonest. They couldn’t have been bought to swear this?”

===Chinese===
「没有理由认为餐馆里那四个人不老实。他们总不致是受了钱，才赌咒发誓这么说的吧？」

===Original===
“It’s possible, I suppose, but I fear there’s no evidence of it, and even if it were true, we would never get evidence.”

===Chinese===
「我想不无可能，只是恐怕拿不出证据；而且就算真有其事，我们也永远查不到证据。」

===Original===
“In that case, as Austin wasn’t there, they must have been mistaken.”

===Chinese===
「那样的话，既然奥斯汀没去，就一定是他们认错了人。」

===Original===
She looked steadily in Jimmy’s eyes as if challenging him to contest her statement. He marvelled at the faith a good woman will show in the man she loves, and he felt if Austin had by word or deed deceived her, hanging would be too good for him. He hesitated in replying, and she went on:

===Chinese===
她直直地望着吉米的眼睛，仿佛向他挑战，要他驳倒她的话。他惊叹一个善良女子对所爱之人竟有这样的信心；他心想，倘若奥斯汀当真欺骗过她——无论言语还是行动——那就是把他绞死也便宜了他。他迟疑着没有作答，她又接着说：

===Original===
“You understand what I mean? Austin was supposed to have been seen at the boathouse, and as he wasn’t there we deduced an impersonator. We find, in my opinion, the same thing here﻿—probably the same man.”

===Chinese===
「你明白我的意思吧？奥斯汀据说被人看见在船屋，既然他没去，我们便推断有人假扮了他。依我看，这一次还是同一回事——多半还是同一个人。」

===Original===
“In the boathouse case we imagined Cosgrove was the impersonator. Here it could not be Cosgrove, as he was present also.”

===Chinese===
「船屋那一次，我们猜想假扮者是科斯格罗夫。这一回却不可能是他，因为他自己也在场。」

===Original===
She nodded.

===Chinese===
她点了点头。

===Original===
“That is true certainly. Tell me honestly, Jimmy, what you think yourself.”

===Chinese===
「那固然不假。吉米，老老实实告诉我，你自己是怎么想的。」

===Original===
Jimmy hedged.

===Chinese===
吉米支吾其词。

===Original===
“It’s not what I think, Lois, or for the matter of that, what you think, or even what Tanner thinks; it’s what the jury will think; and as you’ve asked me the direct question, I must tell you I greatly fear they will disbelieve Austin.”

===Chinese===
「洛伊丝，问题不在我怎么想，也不在你怎么想，甚至不在坦纳怎么想，而在陪审团怎么想。你既然直截了当问到了我，我只好告诉你：我非常担心，他们不会相信奥斯汀。」

===Original===
“I fear so too,” she answered quietly. He felt she was conscious he had not answered her question, and was thankful she was going to let it pass. But his relief was short-lived.

===Chinese===
「我也怕是这样。」她平静地答道。他觉察出她心里明白他并没有回答她的问题，暗自庆幸她打算放过这一层。可他这点宽心没能维持多久。

===Original===
“You thought he was”﻿—she hesitated for a moment﻿—“not telling you all he might?”

===Chinese===
「你觉得他，」她迟疑了一下，「有些话没有对你和盘托出？」

===Original===
Jimmy hated doing business in opposition to a clever woman. Again and again he had found that except for their own purposes they seldom considered either his words or actions, but always his quite private and secret thoughts. He realised that Lois knew exactly what was in his mind regarding Austin.

===Chinese===
吉米最怕同聪明的女人打交道时站在对立面。他一次又一次地发现，这种女人除非为了她们自己的目的，很少理会他说了什么、做了什么，却总是盯住他内心深处秘而不宣的想法。他意识到，关于奥斯汀，他心里那点盘算，洛伊丝摸得一清二楚。

===Original===
“To be strictly truthful,” he answered, “I admit he did give me the impression that he was holding something back. But of course it was only an impression, and I may have been wrong.”

===Chinese===
「严格说句实话，」他答道，「我承认，他的确给了我一种有所隐瞒的印象。不过那毕竟只是印象，也许是我错了。」

===Original===
She nodded slowly and then said, “I think, Jimmy, I must see him myself.”

===Chinese===
她缓缓点头，随后说道：「吉米，我想，我得亲自去见见他。」

===Original===
This was what her cousin had feared, and he felt he must exert all his powers of diplomacy to prevent it.

===Chinese===
她这位表哥担心的正是这一着，他觉得必须使出全副圆滑手腕，拦住她这一步。

===Original===
“Well, you know, Lois,” he answered truthfully, “I had that in my mind. I hardly liked to suggest it. But undoubtedly if he does know anything, he would tell you when he mightn’t tell me.”

===Chinese===
「呃，是这样，洛伊丝，」他如实答道，「我心里也想到过这一层，只是不大好意思开口提议。不过毫无疑问，他若是真知道些什么，连我都不告诉，也会告诉你的。」

===Original===
She looked at him in unveiled surprise, but only said:

===Chinese===
她毫不掩饰地露出了惊讶的神色，却只说了一句：

===Original===
“Can you arrange an interview for tomorrow?”

===Chinese===
「你能安排明天见面吗？」

===Original===
“I would try if you thought that would be best. But I was going to suggest waiting until Tanner has investigated the affairs of Douglas. He believes, and I agree with him, that there was some private business between Douglas and Sir William, which, if we knew it, would clear up the whole affair.”

===Chinese===
「你若觉得那样最好，我就去试试。不过我正想提议，不妨等坦纳查完道格拉斯的事务再见面。他相信——我也同意——道格拉斯和威廉爵士之间有一桩私下的交易，我们若能探知内情，全案便可真相大白。」

===Original===
“Ah,” said Lois comprehendingly.

===Chinese===
「啊。」洛伊丝会意地说。

===Original===
“If Austin,” Jimmy went on desperately, “is really holding anything back, we may take it he has a good reason for doing so. Unless it becomes really necessary﻿—and it has not, so far﻿—it would be better not to try to force his confidence. He will tell us when he thinks it right.”

===Chinese===
「倘若奥斯汀，」吉米豁出去似的接着说，「当真有什么瞒着，那我们可以认定，他这样做自有充分的理由。除非真到了必要的时候——到目前为止还没有——最好不要强逼他吐露实情。等他觉得时机对了，他自然会告诉我们。」

===Original===
“Really, Jimmy,” Lois smiled faintly, “you are quite coming on. I don’t say you have persuaded me, but I will agree to postpone my visit﻿—shall we say for a week?”

===Chinese===
「真的，吉米，」洛伊丝淡淡一笑，「你倒是越来越长进了。我不敢说你把我说服了，不过我同意把探望往后放一放——就算一星期，怎么样？」

===Original===
“When Tanner returns from Devonshire I shall see him, and let you know his report immediately,” returned the relieved but suspicious Daunt.

===Chinese===
「等坦纳从德文郡回来，我就去见他，一有他的报告，立刻告诉你。」松了口气却仍有疑虑的多恩特答道。

===Original===
They continued discussing the affair for some time. Jimmy could see that in spite of the brave face Lois put on things, she was deeply worried and despondent. Never had he admired her more. He marvelled at her belief in Austin, her assurance that he, Jimmy, was doing the utmost possible, her fairness to Tanner, and her utter and absolute forgetfulness of herself. As he saw her to the train he felt his resolution strengthened, to spare himself neither time nor money to bring about the result she desired.

===Chinese===
两人又把这件事商量了一阵。吉米看得出，洛伊丝尽管强作镇定，心里却忧惧交加、意气消沉。他从来没有这样敬佩过她。他惊叹她对奥斯汀的信念，惊叹她笃信他吉米已竭尽所能，惊叹她对坦纳的公道，更惊叹她把自己忘得干干净净、全然不计。送她上火车时，他只觉决心愈发坚定：为促成她所盼望的结果，时间金钱，在所不惜。

---

===Original===
When Tanner left Daunt’s office on the previous day, he returned at once to the Yard. First he arranged for Cosgrove to be shadowed, in case that gentleman, learning of Douglas’s arrest, might consider discretion the better part of valour and disappear. Then he busied himself in reexamining the witnesses of Cosgrove’s movements on the night of the murder, which the efforts of Lois and Daunt had unearthed. When he had heard their statements he had to admit himself convinced of the cousin’s duplicity.

===Chinese===
前一天坦纳离开多恩特的办公室后，径自回了苏格兰场。他先安排人盯住科斯格罗夫，以防那位先生听说道格拉斯被捕，识时务地为保全自己而一走了之。随后他便着手重新查讯洛伊丝和多恩特辛苦挖掘出的那批证人——他们证明的是科斯格罗夫案发当晚的行踪。听过他们的陈述，他不得不承认：这位堂兄的两面伎俩，不由他不信。

===Original===
After a consultation with his chief a warrant was issued, and Tanner went to the flat in Knightsbridge and executed it. When cautioned, Cosgrove made no statement beyond earnestly and emphatically protesting his innocence, and declaring that a terrible mistake had been made.

===Chinese===
与上司商议之后，逮捕令签发了下来，坦纳前往骑士桥的公寓执行。受到法定告诫后，科斯格罗夫除了一迭连声、斩钉截铁地坚称自己清白、断言出了可怕的误会之外，什么供述也没有做。

===Original===
A detailed search of the flat revealed one or two things which Tanner had not already known. As he had suspected on the occasion of his first visit, Cosgrove had a second desk for his more private papers. In the dressing-room was an old Sheraton escritoire, and there the Inspector found complete information about his prisoner’s finances. The latter appeared even more involved than Tanner had suspected, which of course strengthened the motive for the murder, and therefore the case against the accused.

===Chinese===
对公寓的细致搜查，果然搜出了一两件坦纳先前不知道的事。正如他头一回来访时所疑心的，科斯格罗夫另有一张书桌，存放更为私密的文件。更衣室里摆着一张谢拉顿式的旧写字台，探长就在那里查到了这位囚徒财务状况的全部底细。其财务之纠葛，比坦纳疑心的还要深重，这自然加重了谋杀的动机，也就加重了压在被告头上的罪嫌。

===Original===
But this was not all. The motive had been stronger than any merely financial embarrassment could have made it. In the same desk was a bundle of letters from the actress at the Follies, Miss Betty Belcher. These showed that Cosgrove’s relations with her had been extremely intimate. For a considerable time he had evidently been pressing her to marry him, and in one letter, dated about three weeks before Sir William’s death, she had openly admitted she loved him and would marry him if only he were rich. “You know, Cos.,” the rather cynical letter went on, “it would be absurd for me to think of marrying a poor man. I have been too long accustomed to all that money gives to contemplate any other kind of life. If you had a fortune﻿—well, I might consider it, but as things are you must see it would be out of the question.”

===Chinese===
然而还不止于此。这桩罪行的动机，比单凭财务窘迫所能形成的要强烈得多。同一张写字台里，还有一捆「福利斯」剧院那位女演员——贝蒂·贝尔彻小姐——的来信。这些信表明，科斯格罗夫同她的关系已亲密到了极点。显然，相当长一段时间以来，他一直在催她嫁给他；其中一封写于威廉爵士死前约三个星期，她坦白承认自己爱他，只要他有钱就嫁。「你是知道的，科斯，」这封颇见凉薄的信接着写道，「叫我去想嫁给一个穷光蛋，未免太荒唐了。钱财给得起的日子，我过得太久啦，别样的生活我想都不愿去想。你要是有笔家私——嗯，我倒可以考虑考虑；可照眼下的光景，你总该如何，这是根本办不到的吧。」

===Original===
“He must have been far gone to want to marry her after that,” mused Tanner, “but he evidently did, for here a week later is another letter in the same strain.”

===Chinese===
「挨了这么一封信，还非娶她不可，他准是昏了头。」坦纳暗自沉吟，「可他显然就是这么打算的，你瞧，隔了一个星期，又有一封同样腔调的信。」

===Original===
He filed the papers in the Cosgrove dossier, from which they duly found their way into the hands of the public prosecutor.

===Chinese===
他把这些文件归入科斯格罗夫的案卷，不久，它们便顺理成章地送到了公诉人的手里。

===Original===
The next item on Tanner’s list was a similar search in Douglas’s cottage, and on this business the detective found himself once more seated in the 10:30 a.m. from Paddington, on his second journey to Devonshire.

===Chinese===
坦纳清单上的下一项，是对道格拉斯的小屋做一番同样的搜查。为办这件事，这位侦探又一次坐上了上午 10:30 从帕丁顿开出的列车，开始了他的第二次德文郡之行。

===Original===
He thought he was beginning to get some kind of grasp of the case. It was evident that Austin and Cosgrove, separately and individually, had each the two strongest motives known to weak humanity for desiring Sir William Ponson’s death. In each case there was the direct want of money. But in each case also, to this crude desire was added the more subtle and infinitely more powerful consideration that the money was for the loved one. Neither man could accomplish the marriage upon which he had set his heart, and live afterwards in the way he wished, without more money, and by Sir William’s death this money could alone be obtained.

===Chinese===
他觉得，自己总算开始摸到这案子的门径了。事情很清楚：奥斯汀和科斯格罗夫各自单独来看，人人都怀有软弱人类心目中最强烈的两种动机，盼着威廉·庞森爵士死。其一，是赤裸裸的缺钱。但在这之上，各人又添了一层更微妙、也强大得无以复加的考量——这钱是为了心爱之人的。两人谁都无法在没有更多钱的情况下，成全自己一心向往的婚事，并且婚后按心愿的方式生活；而这笔钱，唯有威廉爵士一死才能到手。

===Original===
So much was obvious, but the facts seemed to permit a further conclusion. Suppose these two, knowing of each other’s position, had conspired together to commit the crime which would relieve the necessities of both? In some way not yet clear they had lured Sir William to the boathouse, met him there, committed the murder, and arranged the matter of the boat to create the impression of accident. In case suspicion should be aroused, each had worked out a false but ingenious alibi.

===Chinese===
以上这些是明摆着的，但事实似乎还容许再进一步推想。假如这两人彼此知道对方的处境，索性合谋干了这桩能同时解救两人困厄的罪行呢？他们以某种尚未查明的法子，把威廉爵士诱到船屋，在那里同他会面，下了毒手，再布置好那条小船，做出失事的样子。为了防备疑心惹起，又各自编造了一套虽属捏造却颇巧妙的不在场证明。

===Original===
Tanner felt himself so far on fairly firm ground, but when he came to consider Douglas and the part he had played in the affair, he had to confess himself absolutely at sea. However, the search on which he was now engaged might throw some light on that.

===Chinese===
到这一步，坦纳自觉脚下还算站得稳；可一想到道格拉斯，想到他在这件事里扮演的角色，他只好承认自己全然如坠云雾。不过，他眼下正着手的那番搜查，也许能照亮一角。

===Original===
He reached Yelverton at the same time as on his first visit, and went at once to the police station. The sergeant had got together some information for him. Douglas, it appeared, had come to the neighbourhood some seven years previously from, the sergeant believed, New York. He had taken a ten-year lease of Myrtle Cottage, had engaged an elderly housekeeper who was still with him, and had settled down to a quiet existence of gardening and bee farming. That he had some money was obvious, but he was not well off, and seemingly had at first found it difficult to make ends meet. But during the last four years his prospects appeared to have improved, as he had carried out a number of alterations to the house, had purchased a small car, and generally seemed to have taken things more easily. The sergeant, after Tanner had left on the day of the attempted arrest, had made a careful search of the house, but without finding anything suspicious. He had then admitted the housekeeper, who had been visiting friends in Princetown, and she had been living there since. Douglas had not borne a very lofty reputation in the neighbourhood. He was morose and ill-tempered, and drank more than was good for him. But he kept himself to himself and there had been no open disputes with his neighbours.

===Chinese===
他同头一回来时一样的时候到了耶尔弗顿，径直去了警察所。警佐已替他汇集了一些情况。据查，道格拉斯约七年前来到这一带，警佐相信，是从纽约（New York）来的。他租下桃金娘小屋，一租十年，雇了一位上了年纪的女管家——此人至今仍在他家——便安顿下来，过起种花养蜂的清净日子。他手头显然有几个钱，但算不上富裕，起先似乎还捉襟见肘、入不敷出。可近四年来，境况显然好转了：他把房子翻修了好几处，买了一辆小汽车，总之日子过得宽裕了些。逮捕未遂的那天，坦纳走后，警佐把屋子仔细搜了一遍，没发现任何可疑之物。随后他放回了那位去普林斯敦访友的女管家，此后她便一直住在那里。道格拉斯在这一带的名声并不怎么样。他郁郁寡欢，脾气暴躁，贪杯误事。但他独来独往，同邻居也没有公开结过怨。

===Original===
So much Tanner knew when he reached the house to conduct his own examination.

===Chinese===
坦纳到达那所屋子着手亲自查勘时，掌握的情况就是这些。

===Original===
A lengthy interrogation of the housekeeper led to nothing fresh. And then began another of those exhaustive searches to which Tanner was so well accustomed, and which always bored him so exceedingly.

===Chinese===
对女管家盘问了半天，没有问出半点新东西。接着便开始了那种翻箱倒柜的大搜查——这种活计坦纳早已驾轻就熟，也一向腻烦得很。

===Original===
He found nothing of interest till he came to examine Douglas’s papers, but from them he learned a good deal of the man’s life. Douglas had been, it was evident, a clerk in the Pennsylvania Railroad Terminal in New York, there being letters on railway paper and photographs of groups of employees, as well as a testimonial from the head of the office. This was dated seven years earlier, and referred to Douglas’s service of twenty-one years. The man must therefore have held the position since 1892. Of his life since settling in Devonshire there were records, principally connected with beekeeping, but of his history before his connection with the Pennsylvania Railroad Tanner could discover nothing.

===Chinese===
直到着手检查道格拉斯的文件，他才算找到有意思的东西，并由此摸清了此人生平的大概。看得出，道格拉斯从前是纽约宾夕法尼亚铁路车站（Pennsylvania Railroad Terminal）的一名职员：有印着铁路公司信头的信件，有职员合影的照片，还有一封办公室主管出具的服务证明信。证明信落款于七年前，说的是道格拉斯二十一年的服务经历。如此算来，此人自 1892 年起便一直担任此职。至于他定居德文郡以后的生活，倒有些记录，主要是养蜂方面；但在进入宾夕法尼亚铁路之前的经历，坦纳却查不出丝毫。

===Original===
“He is surely either an Englishman or a wonderful mimic,” thought the Inspector, as he recalled the north-country accent with which the man had spoken on the day of his bolt for liberty.

===Chinese===
「他要么是个英国人，要么就是个模仿奇才。」探长心想，耳边又响起那人夺路逃亡那天说话时那口北方口音。

===Original===
The search dragged on, and at last, as it was nearly concluded, Tanner made three finds, though none of them seemed of much value. The first was that when examining with a mirror the blotting paper on Douglas’s desk, he saw that an envelope had been addressed to Sir William Ponson. Unfortunately, in spite of his careful efforts he could trace nothing of the letter presumably sent therein, but the marks were a still further proof of the relations which had obtained between the two men.

===Chinese===
搜查磨磨蹭蹭地进行着，到快要收尾时，坦纳有了三样发现，尽管哪一样看上去都不甚值钱。头一样：他用一面小镜子照看道格拉斯书桌上的吸墨纸，看出曾有一个信封是写给威廉·庞森爵士的。可惜，任凭他怎样细心，也辨不出想必装在里面的那封信写了什么；但那几道印痕，进一步证明了两人之间确有往来。

===Original===
The second discovery appeared at first sight of even less importance, and Tanner noted it principally as being the only thing he had yet come on which, it seemed possible, might refer to Douglas’s early life. In an old and apparently little used book on American passenger rates, the leaves of which the Inspector was painfully turning over in the hope that some old letter might lie therein concealed, he came on a photograph. Evidently of considerable age, it was faded to a light brown and discoloured as if at some time it had been wet. It was a view of a tombstone and grave with a building﻿—presumably the porch of a church﻿—in the background. A lich-gate showed in the farther distance, while on the stone the inscription appeared as dark, broken lines, the only word decipherable being the first﻿—“Sacred.” Tanner put the photograph in his pocket with the idea that this might represent Douglas’s family burying ground, which, if traceable, might throw light on his birthplace. At the same time he felt that such information, even if obtainable, could not help much in his quest.

===Chinese===
第二样发现乍看更无关紧要；坦纳之所以记下它，主要是因为这是他迄今所遇唯一一件或许涉及道格拉斯早年生活的东西。在一本论述美国客运票价、显然久已不翻的旧书里——探长一页一页费劲地翻检，指望里面会夹着什么旧信——他翻出了一张照片。照片显然年代久远，褪成了浅褐色，斑斑渍渍，仿佛曾经浸过水。拍的是一块墓碑和坟茔，背景里有一座建筑——想必是教堂的门廊。更远处显出一座墓园门楼（lich-gate），碑上的铭文则化作一行行断续的黑线，唯一认得出的字是开头那个——「Sacred」。坦纳把照片揣进口袋，心想这也许拍的是道格拉斯家的祖坟，若能查实，或可探明他的出生地。同时他又觉得，这类情况即便查得到，对破案也帮不了什么大忙。

===Original===
The third find was that in an engagement book or diary there was a reference to the visit to London, and to certain calls to be paid there. On the space for the Thursday before the murder was written “London, 10:25 train, Judd’s Hotel, Dunlop Street.” On the next space, for Friday, was an entry, “Insurance Co., 77B Gracechurch St.” There was a list of articles﻿—probably purchases﻿—“Collars, handkerchiefs, *The Apiarist*, by S. Wilson Holmes,” and some other items. Last, but not least, for the evening of the murder there was “X﻿—9:30 p.m.”

===Chinese===
第三样发现在一本记事簿或日记簿里：上面记着那次伦敦之行，以及要在伦敦办的几处拜访。案发前那个星期四的栏里写着：「伦敦，10:25 的车，贾德旅馆，邓禄普街。」下一格是星期五，记着一条：「保险公司，格雷教堂街 77B。」还有一张单子——多半是要买的东西——「领子、手帕、《养蜂人》（The Apiarist），S. 威尔逊·霍尔姆斯（S. Wilson Holmes）著」，另有一些别的名目。最后，也是顶要紧的一笔，案发当晚那一栏写着：「X——晚上 9:30。」

===Original===
This last entry set Tanner puzzling. “X,” he presumed, stood for the Luce Manor boathouse, and its use seemed to show the same desire for secrecy about his visit there as had been noticeable with the others who had been present. But Tanner had to confess that this entry did not square with the theory that the murder had been its object﻿—at least on Douglas’s part. It was inconceivable that a man about to commit such a crime should have required a reminder of the hour of the deed. Every detail of the plan would have been seared into his brain. Was the suggestion of this entry, wondered the Inspector, not that Douglas had been made a tool of by the cousins? If the man should make that case this would certainly be corroborative evidence. Tanner attached some weight to the point, as he felt it was too subtle to have been designed.

===Chinese===
最后这一笔让坦纳犯了琢磨。他猜想，「X」指的就是卢斯庄园的船屋；用这样一个代号，同其余到过现场的人如出一辙，显出同样的讳莫如深。可是坦纳不得不承认，这一笔同「此行目的就是谋杀」的推断对不上号——至少在道格拉斯这方面说不通。一个人就要去犯下这样的罪行，竟还需要个条子提醒自己动手的钟点，这于理不通。计划的每个细节，都该深深烙在他脑子里才对。难道这一笔的意味，探长暗想，不是说明道格拉斯是被那对堂兄弟当了枪使吗？倘若那人日后这样辩解，这便是确凿的旁证。坦纳觉得这一条颇有分量，因为想得这么细微，倒不像是事先安排出来的。

===Original===
Having seen from the papers that Douglas had an account in the Plymouth branch of the Western Counties Bank, Tanner next day called on the manager. Here, after a study of the accused’s finances, he made an interesting discovery. At intervals during the last four years Douglas had lodged sums of money﻿—invariably in notes, so he was told﻿—and what particularly intrigued the Inspector’s imagination was the fact that each such lodgment had taken place a few days after the drawing of an “X” cheque by Sir William Ponson, and in each case it was for just a trifle less than the amount of that cheque. It seemed evident that Sir William had been paying Douglas these sums, and the method of lodging showed the latter equally eager to keep the transactions secret. What service, mused Tanner, could Douglas have possibly done Sir William to have merited such a return?

===Chinese===
从文件里看到道格拉斯在西部诸县银行（Western Counties Bank）普利茅斯分行立有账户，坦纳第二天便去拜访经理。他对被告的财务研究了一番之后，有了一个耐人寻味的发现。近四年来，道格拉斯隔三差五存入款项——据告知，回回都是钞票——而尤其令探长浮想联翩的是：每一笔这样的存款，都落在威廉·庞森爵士开出一张「X」支票之后没几天，而且每笔都比那张支票的金额只少那么一星半点。看来显而易见，是威廉爵士一直在付给道格拉斯这些钱；而存款的方式也表明，后者同样急着要把这些往来瞒住。道格拉斯到底替威廉爵士效过什么劳，坦纳寻思，竟值得这样的报偿？

===Original===
It was an anxious and disappointed Inspector who that afternoon stepped into the London train at Millbay Station, Plymouth. He had been hoping for great things from his search of Douglas’s rooms, and he had found practically nothing﻿—only an old photograph and the address of an insurance company in London. And neither of these seemed the slightest use. Could anything be learned by tracing that tombstone or calling at that insurance office? He did not think so.

===Chinese===
那天下午，怀着满腹焦虑和失望，跨进普利茅斯米尔贝车站（Millbay Station）开往伦敦列车的，正是探长本人。他本指望搜查道格拉斯的住所能搜出点大名堂，结果几乎一无所获——只得到一张旧照片和一个伦敦保险公司的地址。而这两样，似乎全都派不上一点用场。追查那块墓碑，或者跑一趟那家保险公司，能问出什么来吗？他认为不能。

===Original===
But more than once he had learnt the folly of neglecting any clue, no matter how slight. Therefore on arrival in London he prepared a circular to be sent to every police station in England. It bore a reproduction of the photograph, together with a paragraph asking if the recipient could identify the place and send in a note of its whereabouts, as well as a copy of the inscription on the tombstone.

===Chinese===
可他不止一次领教过轻视任何线索——哪怕再微细——的害处。因此一到伦敦，他便起草了一份通函，发往英格兰各地的每一家警察所。函上印着那张照片的翻版，另附一段文字，请收函人辨认照片上的地点，如果认得，呈报其所在位置，并抄录碑上铭文。

===Original===
Next morning he set out for 77B Gracechurch Street.

===Chinese===
第二天上午，他动身去了格雷教堂街 77B。

===Original===
A suite of offices on the second floor of a large building bore the legend “The Associated Insurance Company, Limited,” and Tanner, entering, asked for the manager. After a short delay he was shown into the presence of a tall, gaunt man, with iron-grey hair, and tired looking eyes. Tanner introduced himself as an Inspector from the Yard.

===Chinese===
一座大楼三楼的一套办公室门上，标着「联合保险有限公司」（The Associated Insurance Company, Limited）的字样。坦纳进门便求见经理。稍候片刻，他被领进去见一位瘦高个子男人，头发铁灰，眼里带着倦色。坦纳自我介绍，说自己是苏格兰场来的一位探长。

===Original===
“I have called, sir,” he went on, “with reference to a man named William Douglas, a small, elderly man with a grey beard, who lives near Yelverton in Devon. I understand that he has had some dealings recently with your Company. I imagine, but am not certain, that he came here on Friday, the 2nd of July last.”

===Chinese===
「先生，我来是为此事，」他接着说，「关乎一个名叫威廉·道格拉斯的人——矮个子的老头儿，蓄着灰白胡须，住在德文郡耶尔弗顿附近。据我了解，他近来同贵公司有过一番往来。我揣想——但不敢断言——他是在不久前 7 月 2 日星期五到过这里。」

===Original===
“I cannot recall the man myself,” the manager returned. “What is the precise point in question?”

===Chinese===
「这人我想不起来了。」经理答道，「究竟所为何事？」

===Original===
“We have had to arrest him on a serious charge﻿—in fact, that of murder. I am endeavouring to trace his recent history and movements. I want to know if he did call, and if so, on what business.”

===Chinese===
「我们不得不以一项严重罪名逮捕了他——事实上，就是谋杀。」坦纳说，「我正在追查他近来的经历和行踪。我想知道他究竟来没来过；若是来过，办的是什么事。」

===Original===
The manager pressed twice a button on his desk. An elderly clerk answered.

===Chinese===
经理按了两下桌上的铃。一位上了年纪的职员应声而来。

===Original===
“Mr. Jones, do you recall our doing any business recently with a man called William Douglas from Devonshire?”

===Chinese===
「琼斯先生（Mr. Jones），你记不记得我们最近同德文郡一个叫威廉·道格拉斯的人有过业务往来？」

===Original===
“Yes, sir,” the clerk replied. “We were in correspondence about an annuity, but the matter fell through.”

===Chinese===
「记得，先生。」职员答道，「我们为年金的事通过几封信，可惜没谈成。」

===Original===
“This gentleman is Mr. Tanner, an Inspector from Scotland Yard. You might let him have all the particulars he wants.” Then to Tanner, “If, sir, you will go with Mr. Jones, he will tell you everything he can.”

===Chinese===
「这位是坦纳先生，苏格兰场的探长。他要了解什么情况，你都如实告诉他。」然后又对坦纳说：「先生，您跟琼斯先生去，他会把知道的全告诉您。」

===Original===
Mr. Jones led the way to a smaller office, and waved his visitor to a chair.

===Chinese===
琼斯先生领路到一间小些的办公室，摆手请来客入座。

===Original===
“William Douglas?” he said, bending over a vertical file. “Here we are, Mr. Tanner.”

===Chinese===
「威廉·道格拉斯？」他一面说，一面俯身在直立档案柜里翻找，「有了，坦纳先生。」

===Original===
He withdrew a folder, and settling himself at his desk, took out some papers.

===Chinese===
他抽出一个文件夹，在办公桌前坐定，取出几份文件。

===Original===
“Here is the first letter. You will see it is an application from William Douglas of Myrtle Cottage, Yelverton, South Devon, for particulars of annuities. He wanted to purchase one which would bring him in £500 a year. Here is our reply enclosing the information and a form for him to fill in, and here is the form which he returned to us duly filled. You will notice he is aged sixty-six. We then wrote him this letter explaining that the annuity would cost him £4,600, and asking his further instructions. He replied, as you see, to proceed with the matter, and he would send on the cheque in due course. We prepared the necessary documents, but received no further communication from Mr. Douglas until about ten days later we had this note stating that he regretted the trouble he had given, but that he found himself unable to proceed with the matter at present. And so it stands.”

===Chinese===
「这是第一封信。您看，是南德文耶尔弗顿桃金娘小屋的威廉·道格拉斯来信，索取年金的章程细则。他想买一份每年能给他带来 500 英镑进项的年金。这是我们复的信，随函附去有关资料和一张要他填写的表格；这张就是他填妥寄回的表格。您可以看到，他现年六十六岁。随后我们又写了这封信，说明这份年金需费 4,600 英镑，并请示下一步办法。如您所见，他复信说着手办理吧，支票随后就寄来。我们把必要的文件都备好了，可道格拉斯先生从此再无音讯，直到大约十天后来了一张便条，说他抱歉给我们添了麻烦，但他眼下无法办这件事了。事情就搁到了现在。」

===Original===
“Then Douglas didn’t call here?”

===Chinese===
「这么说，道格拉斯没有来过？」

===Original===
“No.”

===Chinese===
「没有。」

===Original===
Tanner was considerably puzzled by this information. As he walked slowly along the Embankment back to the Yard, he racked his brains to understand Douglas’s motive or plan. What had been the ex-clerk’s idea? The figures of his bank account showed that at no time since he came to live at Yelverton had he had more than £600 to his credit. As he could not possibly have paid the four thousand odd himself, where did he expect to raise it?

===Chinese===
这些情况让坦纳大惑不解。他沿着河滨大道（the Embankment）缓步走回苏格兰场，一路绞尽脑汁，想弄明白道格拉斯的动机和打算。这个前职员打的是什么主意？他的银行账户数字表明，自从来耶尔弗顿居住，他名下的存款从没有超过 600 英镑。既然这四千多镑他自己断然拿不出来，他又指望从哪儿筹这笔钱呢？

===Original===
And then a sudden idea flashed into the Inspector’s mind. Sir William Ponson had been paying Douglas sums ranging from £100 to £400 at intervals during the last four years. These sums were all paid by cheques marked “X” on the block. On the day before his death Sir William had written an “X” cheque for £3,000. This cheque had never been cashed.

===Chinese===
猛然间，一个念头在探长脑中一闪。近四年来，威廉·庞森爵士一直零星地付给道格拉斯一笔笔款项，从 100 英镑到 400 英镑不等。这些款子，全是用存根上标着「X」的支票支付的。而死前那天，威廉爵士开出了一张 3,000 英镑的「X」支票。这张支票始终没有兑现。

===Original===
Was there not a connection? Had that £3,000 “X” cheque of Sir William’s not been written for the purpose of paying for Douglas’s annuity? It certainly looked like it. And had the sudden death of Sir William not prevented its being cashed?

===Chinese===
这两者之间，岂非大有干系？威廉爵士那张 3,000 英镑的「X」支票，会不会正是为了替道格拉斯购买年金而开？看样子确实如此。而威廉爵士的猝死，岂不恰好阻断了它的兑现？

===Original===
Of course, the amounts did not tally﻿—the cheque was for £3,000, while the price of the annuity was £4,600. But it was obvious that these sums might represent the different opinions the two men held of what was due. Possibly also negotiations were in progress between them on the point. This was of course guesswork, but at least it would explain the facts.

===Chinese===
当然，数目对不上——支票是 3,000 英镑，年金的价钱是 4,600 英镑。但显而易见，这两个数目也许正代表了两人对「该付多少」各执一词。或许，他们在这上头的交涉还在进行之中。这当然纯属猜测，但至少它能解释眼前的事实。

===Original===
The Inspector walked like a man in a dream as he concentrated his thoughts on the whole circumstances. There seemed just one link of his chain missing﻿—some one point which, if he could find it, would flood the whole of these mysterious happenings with light and make the disconnected facts he had learnt fall into their places like the closing pieces of a jigsaw puzzle. And then suddenly he wondered if he had not got it, as another and more sinister idea occurred to him.

===Chinese===
探长凝神通想全局，走路恍恍惚惚，像在梦中。他那条推理的链条，似乎只差一环——某一个关节点，只要找到它，整桩神秘事件便会豁然开朗，他查知的那些散乱事实，也会像拼图收尾的最后几块，各归其位。忽然间，他又觉得自己兴许已经攥住了它——一个更阴森的念头浮上了心头。

===Original===
What if the business were blackmail? It had a nasty enough look. Could Douglas have got hold of something discreditable in Sir William’s life, and could the latter be paying for his silence?

===Chinese===
倘若这桩交易是敲诈呢？这苗头可够吓人的。会不会是道格拉斯抓住了威廉爵士生平一桩见不得人的事，而后者正在为封口付账？

===Original===
The more Tanner thought over it, the more likely this theory seemed. It would explain the facts generally, as well as the secrecy with which both parties had acted. And yet there were difficulties. This annuity business was a difficulty. From Douglas’s point of view it was easy enough to understand. If the blackmailer thought his receipts were precarious, or if time was reducing or about to reduce the value of the secret, it would be a natural step for him to try to convert his vanishing doles into a fixed and certain income. But Sir William’s motive would be different. His only hold on the preservation of his secret was the expectation on Douglas’s part of sums yet to be paid. If the manufacturer agreed to the annuity his hold would be gone. That he should do so was inconceivable to Tanner. And yet apparently he had. He had at least written the £3,000 cheque.

===Chinese===
坦纳越琢磨，越觉得这个解释像那么回事。它既能大体上说明事实，也能说明双方行事何以那样隐秘。然而也有难处。这年金的事就是一桩难处。站在道格拉斯的立场，倒好理解：敲诈者若觉得进账朝不保夕，或者时间正在削减、眼看就要削光那桩秘密的价值，那么设法把渐将断绝的零星讹款，换成一宗固定而稳妥的收入，便是顺理成章的一步。可威廉爵士的动机就不同了。他保住秘密的唯一指望，全在道格拉斯对日后款项的期盼上。这位实业家若答应了年金，这根牵制对方的线便断了。他竟会答应，在坦纳看来不可思议。可他显然是答应了。至少，他开出了那张 3,000 英镑的支票。

===Original===
But the second difficulty to the blackmail theory was more serious. The wrong man had been murdered! If Douglas had been the victim it would have fitted in well enough. It would have been argued that Sir William had taken a desperate remedy to escape from an intolerable situation. But Sir William’s death would have been the last thing Douglas could have desired. He would never have cut off the source of his income. No; attractive as the blackmail theory had seemed at first, Tanner found its difficulties rather overwhelming.

===Chinese===
但敲诈说的第二重难处更要命。死错了人！倘若死的是道格拉斯，那倒顺理成章：可以说威廉爵士为摆脱无法忍受的处境，使出了孤注一掷的手段。可威廉爵士之死，恰恰是道格拉斯最不希望的事。他绝不肯自断财源。不行；敲诈说虽然起初看着诱人，坦纳却发现它的难处压得人喘不过气来。

===Original===
He had by this time reached the Yard, and sitting down at his desk, he lit a cigar, and continued his ruminations.

===Chinese===
这时他已回到苏格兰场，在办公桌前坐下，点上一支雪茄，继续琢磨。

===Original===
Suppose again that blackmail had been levied, where did Austin and Cosgrove come in? They must in this case obviously have taken sides. Either they must have been assisting Sir William to extricate himself, or else they must have been party to the blackmail.

===Chinese===
再假设敲诈确有其事，那奥斯汀和科斯格罗夫又站在哪一边？这种情形下，他们显然必得各助一方：要么是在帮威廉爵士脱身，要么就是敲诈的同谋。

===Original===
But as Tanner pondered these alternatives, he could not see how either would meet the facts. If the cousins were acting for their relative, they obviously would not have murdered him﻿—it was a contradiction in terms. Here again the wrong man had been killed.

===Chinese===
可这两种设想，坦纳想来想去，哪一种都套不上事实。倘若这对堂兄弟是在替长辈出力，那他们显然不会谋害他——这话本身就自相矛盾。又是死错了人。

===Original===
On the other hand, it was difficult to see how they could have been in league with Douglas against Sir William. Anything discreditable to the manufacturer would react on both the son and nephew, and their threat to make the matter public would therefore hardly be convincing. For their own sakes the cousins would be as anxious as Sir William to keep the thing quiet. It was also clear to Tanner that they would never have put themselves in the power of a man like Douglas. If they had wished to murder Sir William for his money, they would have done so at some time when Douglas would not have witnessed the crime.

===Chinese===
反过来，要说他们同道格拉斯联手对付威廉爵士，也难自圆其说。凡是有损这位实业家名誉的事，必定回过头来砸在儿子和侄子自己头上；他们若威胁要把事情张扬出去，根本吓唬不住人。为自身利害计，这对堂兄弟会跟威廉爵士一样，急欲把事情捂严。坦纳同样看得明白：他们绝不会把自己的把柄交到道格拉斯这样的人手里。他们若真想为钱财谋害威廉爵士，自会挑一个道格拉斯目睹不到罪行的时机下手。

===Original===
So far had Tanner progressed when he realised his argument really was that Sir William could not have been murdered at all! He swore angrily, and went back to see the point from which he had started. Blackmail. It would seem, then, that blackmail could not be the explanation. And yet﻿ ﻿… It was an attractive theory﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
坦纳推理到这个地步，才发觉自己的论断竟然成了：威廉爵士根本不可能被人谋杀！他恼火地咒骂了一声，退回去查看自己出发的那个起点。敲诈。这么说来，敲诈恐怕解释不通。可是……这套理论毕竟诱人……

===Original===
Some days later, rather to Tanner’s surprise, he received from a sergeant of police in the north of England an answer to his circular about the photograph. It read:

===Chinese===
几天之后，坦纳颇有些意外地收到英格兰北部一位警佐的回函，答复的正是他关于那张照片的通函。函上写道：

===Original===
“Sir: We have found the churchyard illustrated in your view attached. It belongs to the Parish Church of Tynwick, a village six miles southeast of Gateshead. The headstone is still standing. It bears the inscription﻿—‘Sacred to the memory of John Dale, aged 53, who departed this life on 4th September 1871, and of Eleanor, his beloved wife, who entered into rest on 25th March 1890, at the age of 67.’ ”

===Chinese===
「敬启者：来函所附照片中的教堂墓园，我等已经寻获。它属于廷威克（Tynwick）教区教堂，该村位于盖茨黑德东南六英里。墓碑尚在。碑上铭文为——『Sacred（谨此志念）约翰·戴尔（John Dale），享年五十三，殁于 1871 年 9 月 4 日；暨其爱妻埃莉诺（Eleanor），卒于 1890 年 3 月 25 日，享年六十七。』」

===Original===
“Gateshead? Dale?” thought the Inspector. “Those names sound familiar.”

===Chinese===
「盖茨黑德？戴尔？」探长寻思，「这两个名字听着耳熟。」

===Original===
