He turned to his notes of the case. And then he got rather a thrill. Gateshead was the place from which Sir William had come to Luce Manor. It was there the deceased gentleman had been born and had spent his life, and where the ironworks he had owned was situated.

===Chinese===
他翻出办案笔记查对。这一查，他不由得心头一凛。盖茨黑德，正是威廉爵士迁来卢斯庄园之前所居之地。那位已故绅士生在那里、大半辈子也在那里度过，他名下的炼铁厂也坐落在那里。

===Original===
And Dale? This was more interesting still. Dale was the name of his wife’s first husband! He had married a Mrs. Ethel Dale. Here at last was a connection between the manufacturer and William Douglas.

===Chinese===
那么戴尔呢？这就更有意思了。戴尔，正是他妻子前夫的姓！他娶的正是埃塞尔·戴尔太太。这位实业家同威廉·道格拉斯之间，总算有了一条联系。

===Original===
But after all was it not a very slender one? What exactly did it amount to? That Douglas had in his possession a photograph of the grave of a man and woman of the same name as Lady Ponson’s first husband, and who lived somewhere in the same locality. Not much to go upon, and yet it was suggestive, and where there had been nothing before, Tanner welcomed it eagerly. Who knew what it mightn’t lead to? He determined he must go to Tynwick and make inquiries.

===Chinese===
可这联系，说到底岂不细若游丝？它究竟算得什么呢？不过是：道格拉斯手里存着一张照片，拍的是一对男女的坟墓，男的与庞森夫人前夫同姓，两人生前又恰好住在同一带地方。凭这一点，实在算不上什么；然而它引人遐想，况且原先一片空白，坦纳自是急切地欢迎它。谁知道它会引出什么呢？他打定主意，必须去一趟廷威克，查访一番。

## XIV · A Dramatic Discovery / XIV · 戏剧性的发现

===Original===
The shades of evening were falling when Inspector Tanner reached Newcastle. He had not been favoured with his usual travelling weather. For the first time since he started work on the Ponson case, the skies had remained all day grey and leaden, and the rain had poured ceaselessly and hopelessly down. It had not been possible to open the carriage windows, and he was tired from so long breathing the stuffy atmosphere of the train.

===Chinese===
坦纳探长抵达纽卡斯尔时，暮色正四合。这一路他没能遇上往常出行的好天气。自接手庞森案以来，天头一回整日灰蒙蒙、阴沉沉的，大雨没完没了、没指望似的倾泻而下。车厢的窗子没法打开，长时间呼吸着火车里浑浊的空气，他已觉困乏。

===Original===
It was too late to do anything that night, but the next morning, which fortunately was fine, he took the train to Tynwick. It was a village of about five hundred inhabitants, an attractive little place, with pleasant creeper-covered cottages, separated from the road by narrow gardens, all ablaze with colour. In the centre was the church, and strolling slowly into the churchyard, Tanner had no difficulty in identifying the spot from which the photograph had been taken. As the sergeant had said, the headstone was still standing, and Tanner paused and reread the inscription of which he already had received a copy.

===Chinese===
当晚做什么都太迟了，所幸第二天早晨天色放晴，他乘火车去了廷威克。那是一座约莫五百居民的村庄，景致宜人，一座座爬满藤蔓的村舍讨人喜欢，舍前窄窄的园圃将屋与路隔开，满园花开得绚烂如火。教堂坐落在村子中央，坦纳缓步踱进教堂墓园，毫不费力就认出了拍摄那张照片的位置。正如警佐所言，墓碑依然立着，坦纳驻足片刻，把那段铭文重又读了一遍——碑文的抄件他早已收到。

===Original===
Close by the churchyard and connected with it by a gate in the dividing wall, stood an old, grey stone house﻿—evidently the vicarage. Tanner pushed open the gate, and walking slowly up to the door, knocked.

===Chinese===
墓园紧旁，隔墙开有一道小门，连着一座灰石砌成的老宅——显见得是牧师住宅。坦纳推开小门，缓步走到宅门前，叩响了门。

===Original===
“Could I see the vicar for a few moments?” he asked courteously, as the door was opened by a trim maid.

===Chinese===
门由一位装束利落的女仆打开，他客气地问道：「能否请牧师先生赏几分钟？」

===Original===
He was shown into a comfortable study, and there after a few moments he was joined by an elderly man, clean shaven, white haired, and kindly looking.

===Chinese===
他被引进一间舒适的书房，片刻之后，一位老者进来与他相见——面庞剃得干干净净，须发花白，神色和善。

===Original===
“Good morning,” said the latter. “You wished to see me?”

===Chinese===
「早上好，」老者说道，「您要见我？」

===Original===
Tanner rose and bowed.

===Chinese===
坦纳起身鞠了一躬。

===Original===
“Yes, sir,” he answered, “for a moment.”

===Chinese===
「是的，先生，」他答道，「只占您片刻。」

===Original===
“Sit down, won’t you?” His host waved him to an armchair and seated himself at his desk.

===Chinese===
「请坐，好吗？」主人抬手让进扶手椅，自己在书桌后落了座。

===Original===
“My business, sir,” went on Tanner, “is, I expect, of a rather unusual kind for you to deal with. My name is Tanner﻿—Inspector Tanner of New Scotland Yard, and I have come to ask your kind help in obtaining some information of which I am in need.”

===Chinese===
「先生，我此来的事情，」坦纳接着说，「想必是您不大经手的那一类。我姓坦纳——新苏格兰场的坦纳探长，此来是想恳请鼎助，替我弄到一些需要的情况。」

===Original===
If the clergyman was surprised he did not show it.

===Chinese===
这位教士若是意外，却丝毫没有显露出来。

===Original===
“And what is the nature of the information?” he asked.

===Chinese===
「那要打听的是什么性质的情况？」他问。

===Original===
Tanner took the photograph from his pocket.

===Chinese===
坦纳从口袋里取出那张照片。

===Original===
“We have had,” he explained, “to arrest a man on suspicion of a serious crime﻿—murder, in fact. The only clue to his antecedents we have is this photograph. You will see it represents part of your churchyard, and the headstone in the foreground is in memory of John Dale and his wife, Eleanor. We thought if we could find out something about these Dales, it might help us.”

===Chinese===
「我们不得已，」他说明道，「逮捕了一名涉嫌重大罪行的男子——事实上是谋杀。我们掌握的关于他来历的唯一线索，就是这张照片。您看，照片拍的正是贵教堂墓园的一角，前景里那块墓碑，是纪念约翰·戴尔及其妻子埃莉诺的。我们想，若能查明这些戴尔家的情况，或许于破案有助。」

===Original===
“Is Dale the name of your suspect?”

===Chinese===
「戴尔是您那位嫌疑犯的姓名？」

===Original===
“No, sir, he is called Douglas, but of course that may not be his real name.”

===Chinese===
「不是，先生，他自称道格拉斯，不过那当然未必是真名。」

===Original===
The clergyman thought for a few moments.

===Chinese===
教士沉吟了片刻。

===Original===
“I fear I cannot tell you very much,” he said at last. “When I came here thirteen years ago there was no one of that name in the parish. I do remember hearing of the family you mention, but they had moved some years previously.”

===Chinese===
「恐怕我帮不上您多少忙，」他终于说道，「十三年前我来此地时，教区里已经没有姓戴尔的人家了。您提的这家人我确实记得听说过，但他们早在几年之前就搬走了。」

===Original===
“You don’t know to where?”

===Chinese===
「您不知道搬去了哪里？」

===Original===
“Unfortunately I do not.”

===Chinese===
「很遗憾，不知道。」

===Original===
“Perhaps, sir, some of your remaining parishioners could tell me?”

===Chinese===
「那么先生，贵教区还有的老住户，可有人能告诉我？」

===Original===
“That’s what I was going to suggest.” The clergyman again paused. “There is a family called Clayton living close by, gentlemen farmers, who have been here for generations. Old Mr. Clayton is well over seventy, but still remains hale and hearty﻿—a wonderful man for his age. I should think that if anyone could give you your information, he could. He’ll probably be at home now, and if you like, I’ll go down with you and introduce you.”

===Chinese===
「我正想这么建议。」教士又顿了顿，「近处住着一户姓克莱顿（Clayton）的人家，是世代居此的乡绅农户。老克莱顿先生早已年过七旬，身子骨却依旧硬朗康健——就他的年纪说真是了不得。我想，若还有人能向您提供那些情况，必是他无疑。这会儿他多半在家，您若愿意，我就陪您走一趟，替您引见。」

===Original===
“I should be more than grateful.”

===Chinese===
「那真是感激不尽。」

===Original===
“Come then,” said the vicar, leading the way.

===Chinese===
「这就请吧。」牧师说着在前引路。

===Original===
The Claytons lived on the outskirts of the village in a charming little creeper-covered house, standing in small but perfectly kept grounds. As the two men passed up the rose-bordered path to the door, they were hailed from the lawn behind. An old gentleman with a full white beard, a grey felt hat, and a tweed suit was approaching.

===Chinese===
克莱顿家住在村子边上，一座爬满藤蔓的漂亮小宅，立在小巧却打理得一丝不苟的庭园里。两人沿玫瑰夹道的小径向门口走去时，后面的草坪上有人招呼他们。一位老绅士正走近前来——白色胡须蓄得满满的，头戴灰色毡帽，身穿花呢衣装。

===Original===
“Mornin’, vicar,” he cried cheerily.

===Chinese===
「早啊，牧师，」他欢快地喊道。

===Original===
“How are you, Mr. Clayton? Beautiful morning. Can we have a word with you?”

===Chinese===
「您好啊，克莱顿先生！早晨多好。能同您说几句话吗？」

===Original===
“Delighted, I’m sure. Come in here. It’s always better out of doors than in, eh, Vicar?”

===Chinese===
「欢迎之至。就这儿坐吧。待在外头总比待在屋里舒坦，对吧，牧师？」

===Original===
He shook hands with the clergyman, and turned expectantly to Tanner.

===Chinese===
他同教士握了握手，又满怀期待地转向坦纳。

===Original===
“May I introduce Mr. Tanner? Mr. Tanner has just called with me in search of some information which I unfortunately was unable to give him, but which I thought you possibly might.”

===Chinese===
「容我引见，坦纳先生。坦纳先生方才随我来访，想打听一些情况，可惜我提供不了，我想您或许是能的。」

===Original===
“I had better introduce myself more fully, Mr. Clayton,” said Tanner. “I am a detective officer from Scotland Yard, and I am trying to trace a family named Dale, who, I understand, formerly lived here.”

===Chinese===
「我还是把自己的身份说得再透彻些好，克莱顿先生，」坦纳说，「我是苏格兰场的侦探警官，正在查访一户姓戴尔的人家，据我了解，他们从前住在此地。」

===Original===
Mr. Clayton led the way to a delightfully situated arbour, and waved his guests to easy chairs, but the vicar excused himself on the ground that his part in the affair was complete. On his departure Tanner produced the photograph and explained his business to his host.

===Chinese===
克莱顿先生把客人引到一处位置极好的凉棚里，抬手请他们坐进安乐椅；牧师却称自己在这件事里的角色已告完成，告退了。他一走，坦纳便取出照片，向主人道明了来意。

===Original===
“The Dales? Yes, I knew them well. They lived at the other end of the village for many years, until indeed John Dale, the father, died. Then they moved into Gateshead. They weren’t left too well off, I’m afraid. But I don’t know that any of them are alive now.”

===Chinese===
「戴尔家？熟得很。他们在村子那一头住了好多年，直到父亲约翰·戴尔去世，一家才搬进盖茨黑德。恐怕他们没落下多少家产。这一家人如今还有没有在世的，我就不知道了。」

===Original===
“What did the family consist of?”

===Chinese===
「家里都有什么人？」

===Original===
“The mother and two sons. She died some years after her husband﻿—you have the date on your inscription.”

===Chinese===
「母亲和两个儿子。她在丈夫死后几年也过世了——日期碑上有。」

===Original===
“And can you tell me anything about the sons?”

===Chinese===
「那两个儿子，您能讲讲吗？」

===Original===
“Yes, I remember them well. They were very like each other﻿—good looking, with taking manners, well dressed and all that, but a couple of rotters at heart. They were always out for what they could get, and there was drink and gambling and worse. When they cleared out they weren’t much loss.”

===Chinese===
「记得清楚。兄弟俩长得极像——相貌英俊，举止讨喜，衣着一向体面，可惜骨子里是一对无赖。两人一门心思捞取好处，酗酒、赌博，还有更不堪的。他们一走了之，村里也不觉得可惜。」

===Original===
“Place too hot to hold them?”

===Chinese===
「是此地容不下他们了？」

===Original===
“In Edward’s case, I think so. Edward was the younger. He was in debt heavily, I know, and he slipped off quietly one night to the States, and was never heard of again.”

===Chinese===
「爱德华（Edward）嘛，我看是。他是弟弟。我知道他债台高筑，一天夜里悄悄溜去了美国，从此杳无音讯。」

===Original===
“And the other?”

===Chinese===
「那另一个呢？」

===Original===
“The elder brother, Tom, was a bad lot too. He had a tragic end. He was drowned. But I don’t think anyone mourned for him. He had well-nigh broken his young wife’s heart in the three years they were married.”

===Chinese===
「哥哥汤姆也不是好东西。他下场凄惨，是溺死的。不过我看没有人为他哀伤。成婚那三年，他几乎把年轻妻子的心伤透了。」

===Original===
Tanner was like a bloodhound on a hot scent. This was very interesting. He remembered that Sir William Ponson had married a Mrs. Dale from this part of the country, whose husband had been drowned on his way to Canada. It looked like as if the Tom Dale of whom Mr. Clayton had been speaking might have been this man.

===Chinese===
坦纳此刻就像循着热迹的猎犬。这太有意思了。他记得威廉·庞森爵士娶的正是这一带的一位戴尔太太，她前夫是在前往加拿大途中溺亡的。看样子，克莱顿先生方才说起的这位汤姆·戴尔，很可能就是此人。

===Original===
“What was the business of the Dale brothers?” he asked.

===Chinese===
「戴尔兄弟是做什么营生的？」他问。

===Original===
“They were both in the same firm﻿—the Eagle Ironworks. You know it maybe﻿—in Gateshead? It was Peter Howard’s then. I remember young Ponson joining it﻿—poor fellow, he’s gone now﻿—it was he that made it. When he started as office boy there was just one small shed and about a dozen men, and now it’s a company employing over a thousand hands. A wonderful change.”

===Chinese===
「兄弟俩同在一家商号——『雄鹰』炼铁厂（the Eagle Ironworks）。您也许知道——在盖茨黑德？那时厂子还是彼得·霍华德（Peter Howard）的产业。我记得小庞森进厂当差——可怜的人，如今也去了——厂子全是他一手做起来的。他刚进去当办公室小听差时，全厂只有一间小棚屋和十来个人，如今已是一家雇工上千的公司了。了不得的变化。」

===Original===
“Wonderful indeed, Mr. Clayton.”

===Chinese===
「确实了不得，克莱顿先生。」

===Original===
“Ay. A man of my years can look back over great changes. That’s more than a young fellow like you can do, eh, Mr. Tanner?”

===Chinese===
「是啊。我这把年纪的人，回头能望尽这许多沧桑巨变。这可是你们这样的小伙子比不了的，对吧，坦纳先生？」

===Original===
“It’s true, sir. And you say Tom was drowned?”

===Chinese===
「说的是，先生。那么您说汤姆是溺死的？」

===Original===
“Yes. He got a sudden call. He was in the *Numidian*. You wouldn’t remember about her?”

===Chinese===
「是啊。他是骤然蒙主宠召的。他乘的是『努米底亚人』号。那船的事，您不记得了吧？」

===Original===
“I don’t think so.”

===Chinese===
「怕是不记得。」

===Original===
“No, it would be before your time. A terrible business it was. The *Numidian* was a big boat, big for those times, I mean. She was running from Glasgow to Quebec, and she struck a berg. Went down off the banks in a few minutes. Nearly every soul on board was lost, and Tom Dale was one of them. A sudden call, it was.”

===Chinese===
「是啊，那还在您出生之前。一场大祸啊。『努米底亚人』号是条大船——我是说按那个年头算大。她跑的是格拉斯哥（Glasgow）到魁北克（Quebec）的航线，撞上了冰山，几分钟内就在大浅滩外海沉没了。船上几乎无一生还，汤姆·戴尔就是其中之一。真是骤蒙天召啊。」

===Original===
“A terrible affair. I do remember hearing of it.”

===Chinese===
「惨事啊。我倒确实记得听说过。」

===Original===
“Ay, no doubt. A sudden call for Tom, that it was.”

===Chinese===
「是啊，没错。对汤姆来说，就是骤然蒙召啊。」

===Original===
“You said he nearly broke a good woman’s heart, Mr. Clayton?”

===Chinese===
「您方才说他险些伤透了一位好女人的心，克莱顿先生？」

===Original===
“Ay, and so he did. Little Ethel Osborne was fool enough to marry him. And it wasn’t long till she was sorry for it. They say she saw him drunk for the first time the night after the wedding. But it wasn’t the last, not by a long chalk. He was a bad boy all through, was Tom.”

===Chinese===
「是啊，伤透了。小埃塞尔·奥斯本鬼迷心窍，竟嫁给了他。没多久她就悔了。据说婚后第二天夜里，她头一回见他喝得烂醉。可那绝不是最后一回，远远不是。汤姆这人是坏到底了。」

===Original===
“Then his death must have been something of a release to her?”

===Chinese===
「那么他的死，对她多少算是一种解脱吧？」

===Original===
“Yes, poor soul. But she had more sense the second time.”

===Chinese===
「是啊，可怜的人。不过第二回她可就明白多了。」

===Original===
“The second time?”

===Chinese===
「第二回？」

===Original===
“Ay, she did what she ought to have done at the start﻿—married young William Ponson.”

===Chinese===
「是啊，她做了当初本就该做的事——嫁给了年轻的威廉·庞森。」

===Original===
“Never neglect the smallest clue!” thought Tanner triumphantly, as he recalled his doubt of the wisdom of following up the photograph. The connection between Douglas and Sir William was strengthening. Doubtless he was on the right track at last, and maybe if he questioned him skilfully, this old man would let something drop which would give away the secret.

===Chinese===
「再小的线索也万万不可轻忽！」坦纳得意地想道，一面记起自己先前还怀疑追查这张照片是否明智。道格拉斯与威廉爵士之间的联系正在坐实。毫无疑问，他终于走上正路了；说不定，只消巧加引导，这位老人就会漏出什么话来，把秘密捅破。

===Original===
Mr. Clayton was glad to talk﻿—the old gentleman seemed lonely﻿—and presently the whole story came out. Substantially it was the same as that Tanner had already heard from Mr. Arbuthnot, the late manufacturer’s lawyer. Mr. Clayton told of William Ponson’s start in life as office-boy in the Eagle Ironworks of John Howard; of his rapid rise to the position, first of manager, then partner, and finally of sole owner; of his taking his brother John, Cosgrove’s father, into the concern; of their extraordinary prosperity; of William’s municipal life, culminating in his knighthood, and of John’s death, followed by Sir William’s sale of the business, and retirement to Luce Manor.

===Chinese===
克莱顿先生乐意说话——这位老先生看来是寂寞的——不消片刻，事情的原委便和盘托出。大体上，这与坦纳此前从已故实业家的律师阿巴思诺特先生那里听来的相同。克莱顿先生讲起威廉·庞森早年在约翰·霍华德（John Howard）的「雄鹰」炼铁厂当办公室小听差起家；讲他如何一路升迁，先当经理，继而合伙人，终于独资东家；讲他把弟弟约翰——科斯格罗夫的父亲——带进厂中；讲两人生意如何出奇兴隆；讲威廉的市政生涯，直至荣膺爵士；讲约翰亡故后，威廉爵士盘出产业，退居卢斯庄园。

===Original===
With all of this Tanner was familiar, but he found Mr. Clayton was able to give him rather more details of the manufacturer’s family affairs than he had yet learnt.

===Chinese===
这些坦纳都已了然，但他发现，关于这位实业家的家事，克莱顿先生还能给他讲出一些至今未曾听闻的细节。

===Original===
It seemed that when the deceased knight was aged seven-and-twenty, he had fallen deeply in love with a Miss Ethel Osborne, the daughter of a Gateshead doctor. Miss Osborne was a pretty, though not very brilliant girl of some twenty summers, with a placid, pleasure-loving disposition, and a little money. The Dale brothers at this time held positions in the firm, Tom, the elder being a traveller, and his brother Edward a clerk. Tom was a handsome youth with rather fascinating manners. He was considerably below middle height, had delicate features, small and beautifully shaped hands and feet, and dark, passionate eyes.

===Chinese===
原来，这位已故爵士二十七岁那年，曾深深爱上了一位埃塞尔·奥斯本小姐，是盖茨黑德一位医生的女儿。奥斯本小姐年方二十上下，人虽算不得十分聪慧，却生得俊俏，性情恬静，贪恋逸乐，还带着一点薄产。那时戴尔兄弟都在厂里任职：哥哥汤姆当旅行推销员，弟弟爱德华当文书。汤姆是个俊俏青年，举止颇具魅力。他身量比中等个头矮上一大截，眉目清秀，手足纤细有致，一双眼睛乌黑热烈。

===Original===
When William Ponson began to press his attentions on Ethel Osborne, he soon found he had a rival in Tom Dale. For a long time the young lady was unable to decide between her two admirers. For Ponson she had more respect, and she felt that as his wife she would have an assured position and a comfortable home. But Ponson was “stodgy.” His thoughts were centred in his work, and his own advancement, and he had forsworn that lighter side of life﻿—theatres, dances, excursions﻿—which the young girl found so attractive. With Dale she believed her prospects might be less secure, but life would certainly be pleasanter. He seemed to understand her, and respond to her moods better than the other, and he was a delightful companion. And who shall blame her if she sacrificed material prosperity to the joy of life, rather, who shall not praise her?

===Chinese===
威廉·庞森开始向埃塞尔·奥斯本殷勤求爱之后，很快发现自己有了个情敌，便是汤姆·戴尔。有很长一段时间，这位年轻小姐在两位倾慕者之间委决不下。对庞森，她怀有更多的敬重，她也觉得做他的妻子，地位有保障，家境安稳舒适。可庞森「乏味」。他一心扑在工作和个人前程上，早已戒绝了生活中轻快的那一面——看戏、跳舞、郊游——而那些恰是这位少女觉得最叫人心动的。跟了戴尔，她认为前景也许不那么牢靠，日子却必定更痛快。他似乎更懂得她，比另一位更能应和她的心绪，何况他是个讨人喜欢的伴侣。她若为了人生的欢愉而舍弃物质的丰足，谁还忍心责怪她？不如说，谁不该称许她呢？

===Original===
In due time she married Dale, and at once, on the very selfsame day, her disillusionment began. That night, as has already been mentioned, he returned drunk to the Scottish hotel at which they were to spend the honeymoon. And that was only the first occasion of many. Soon she learned of an entanglement with a barmaid which had been going on at the very time of the wedding. It was not long before their numerous quarrels led to an open rupture, and Dale made no secret to his wife of the fact that he had married her for her money. Matters went from bad to worse, till debt began to fasten on them its horrid shackles, and ruin stared them in the face. The one alleviating circumstance was that there had been no children from the marriage.

===Chinese===
到头来她嫁给了戴尔，而就在同一天，她的幻梦当即开始破灭。当夜，正如前文已经提过的，他喝得烂醉，回到两人本要共度蜜月的那家苏格兰旅馆。而这还只是无数次中的头一遭。不久她就听说，他与一名酒吧女招待早有纠缠，且恰恰延续到婚期前后。没过多久，两口子无休无止的争吵演成公开决裂，戴尔对妻子也毫不讳言：他图的正是她的钱。境况每况愈下，直到债务把可怖的枷锁套上两人，败落在眼前逼视他们。唯一聊可宽慰的是，这桩婚姻没有留下一儿半女。

===Original===
All this William Ponson watched, grieving for Ethel, but of course helpless. Then it became necessary for the firm to send a representative to Canada, and the choice fell on their traveller, Tom Dale. Whether or not Ponson had any say in this decision was not known, but at all events Dale sailed for Quebec in the *Numidian*. As will be remembered, the vessel was lost off the coast of Newfoundland, a mere handful of her complement being saved. Dale’s name was amongst the lost. Ethel Dale therefore found herself not only without assets, but called upon to meet a considerable crop of debts. Her father having died since her marriage, she was thus absolutely destitute.

===Chinese===
这一切，威廉·庞森都看在眼里，为埃塞尔痛心，却又无可奈何。后来商行需要派一名代表去加拿大，人选落在他们的旅行推销员汤姆·戴尔头上。庞森在这项决定中有无发言权，无人知晓，但无论如何，戴尔乘「努米底亚人」号航向魁北克去了。如前所述，该船在纽芬兰外海失事，全船人员仅寥寥数人获救。戴尔名列遇难者之中。于是埃塞尔·戴尔发现自己不但身无分文，还须清偿一大串债务。她父亲又已在她婚后去世，她遂陷于赤贫，山穷水尽。

===Original===
It was believed to be owing to William Ponson’s efforts that a small pension was granted her by the firm, and the debts were wiped out by a presentation from some of the employees. She took a small house, and by letting rooms contrived to make a living.

===Chinese===
据说，正是亏得威廉·庞森出力，商行才发给她一小笔抚恤金；那笔债务，则由部分员工凑了一份礼金代为清偿。她赁下一所小宅，靠分租房间勉强糊口。

===Original===
William Ponson, though he had acted throughout in a strictly honourable manner, had never ceased to love Ethel. He bided his time for over two years, then, calling on the widow, he told her of his love and boldly pressed his suit. She then realised that she had loved him all along, and though at first she refused to consider his proposal, his steady insistence wore down her opposition and in 1887, five years after he had first loved her, he obtained his desire, and they were married. The trouble through which she had passed had profoundly modified her character, sobering her and bringing out all that was best in her, and her life with William Ponson, though quiet, had been truly happy. Two children were born, Austin and Enid.

===Chinese===
威廉·庞森虽自始至终行止光明磊落，对埃塞尔的爱恋却从未断绝。他静候时机两年有余，而后登门看望这位寡妇，向她倾诉爱意，大胆求婚。她这才明白，自己心里始终爱的是他；起初她不肯考虑他的求婚，但他一以贯之的坚持磨平了她的抗拒。1887 年，即他初坠爱河五年之后，他终于如愿以偿，两人结为夫妻。她经历的那些磨难深深改变了她的性情，使她沉稳下来，把她身上最好的品性引发了出来；她与威廉·庞森的日子虽然平静，却是真正的美满。两人育有一子一女：奥斯汀和伊妮德。

===Original===
“And you said the other Dale went to the States, I think?” asked Tanner, when he had learnt the above facts.

===Chinese===
「您方才说，另一个戴尔去了美国，是吧？」坦纳听完上述种种，问道。

===Original===
“Edward? Yes, he got into difficulties too. He was a born gambler. He was owing money everywhere, and the place got too hot for him. He went to the States shortly after Tom was married.”

===Chinese===
「爱德华？是啊，他也惹上了麻烦。他天生是个赌徒，到处欠债，此地也容不得他了。汤姆成婚后不久，他就去了美国。」

===Original===
Tanner felt he had done well. Almost first shot he had found this Mr. Clayton and obtained information which must prove of the utmost value. But he had stayed chatting to and pumping the old man for an unconscionable time, and he began to express his thanks, preparatory to taking his leave. And then an idea flashed into his mind, and he sat motionless for some moments, thinking.

===Chinese===
坦纳自觉此行大有收获。几乎一出手，就寻着了这位克莱顿先生，弄到了必定极有价值的情况。只是他缠着老人攀谈套话，已经耽搁了长得不像话的工夫，便开始道谢，预备告辞。就在这时，一个念头闪过脑际，他一动不动地坐了片刻，思索起来。

===Original===
“What was the Dale brother, Edward, like in appearance?” he asked, trying to keep the eagerness out of his voice.

===Chinese===
「那位戴尔家的爱德华，相貌如何？」他问道，竭力不让嗓音里透出急切。

===Original===
“Like enough to his brother Tom, but not so good looking, nor with such good manners by a long chalk. But passably well looking for all that.”

===Chinese===
「同他哥哥汤姆一个模子，只是相貌远不及他，举止也差得远。不过话说回来，模样还算周正。」

===Original===
“But was he a small man?”

===Chinese===
「那他身量矮小吗？」

===Original===
“Small? Ay, that he was﻿—like Tom. Both were small men.”

===Chinese===
「矮小？可不是嘛——跟汤姆一样。兄弟俩都是小个子。」

===Original===
Could it be? Edward Dale, a little man with small hands and feet, knowing all about William Ponson’s youth﻿—knowing probably a good deal more than Mr. Clayton had told or perhaps knew﻿—Edward Dale, a clerk, had gone to America and disappeared. William Douglas, a little man with small hands and feet, and apparently knowing intimate facts about Sir William Ponson﻿—William Douglas, a clerk, had come from America, his youthful history being unknown. Could they be one and the same?

===Chinese===
难道真有这种可能？爱德华·戴尔，一个手足纤细的小个子，熟知威廉·庞森青年时代的一切——多半还知道许多连克莱顿先生都不曾讲出、乃至并不知晓的事——爱德华·戴尔，一名文书，去了美国，从此杳无踪迹。威廉·道格拉斯，一个手足纤细的小个子，显然熟知威廉·庞森爵士的私密隐情——威廉·道格拉斯，一名文书，来自美国，青年经历无人知晓。这两人，会不会本是同一个人？

===Original===
The more Tanner thought over this theory, the more likely it seemed. As he sat smoking with Mr. Clayton in the pleasant garden, he went over in his mind all that he had learnt of each man, and was unable to recall anything inconsistent with the hypothesis.

===Chinese===
坦纳越琢磨这个推断，越觉得可信。他同克莱顿先生坐在宜人的花园里吸烟，把有关这两人各自已知的一切在脑中过了一遍，想不出任何与这假设相抵触之处。

===Original===
But how could he test it? He must make sure. But how?

===Chinese===
可怎样才能验证呢？他必须弄个确实。可是，怎么弄？

===Original===
There was of course one obvious possibility. Mr. Clayton, if confronted with Douglas, might recognise him as Edward Dale. Or Douglas might recognise Mr. Clayton, and so give himself away. It was not a certainty, but it would be worth trying. The Inspector turned to his host.

===Chinese===
当然，有一条显而易见的路子。克莱顿先生若与道格拉斯当面相对，或许能认出他就是爱德华·戴尔。再不然，或许倒是道格拉斯认出克莱顿先生，就此自露马脚。这虽算不得十拿九稳，却值得一试。探长转向主人。

===Original===
“I believe, sir,” he said, “that if I told you just what was troubling me, you might be able to help me out, if you would. I was asking you about Edward Dale, but I did not tell you much about the man we arrested. In the first place, Douglas, as he says his name is, came to England from New York, where he was employed as a clerk in the Pennsylvania Railway for several years. We have traced his movements back to 1892, previous to which we can discover nothing whatever about him. Now, you tell me Edward Dale left for the States about the year 1882, and has since been lost sight of. That is coincidence Number One.”

===Chinese===
「先生，我相信，」他说，「只要我把心里的疑难向您和盘托出，您若愿意，或许能替我解困。方才我向您打听爱德华·戴尔，却没有怎么讲我们逮捕的那个人。首先，那个自称道格拉斯的人是从纽约来英国的，此前他在宾夕法尼亚铁路当了几年文书。我们追查他的行迹，追到 1892 年为止，再往前便一无所获。而您告诉我，爱德华·戴尔约在 1882 年去了美国，此后再无音讯。这是巧合之一。」

===Original===
Mr. Clayton nodded without speaking. He was listening with eager attention.

===Chinese===
克莱顿先生点了点头，没有说话。他正全神贯注地听着。

===Original===
“Next,” continued Tanner, “I did not tell you whose murder the man Douglas was suspected of. It was that of Sir William Ponson.”

===Chinese===
「其次，」坦纳继续道，「我还没有告诉您，道格拉斯涉嫌谋杀的是谁。正是威廉·庞森爵士。」

===Original===
“God bless my soul!” cried the other, “you don’t say so? A terrible affair that. And you think you’ve got the man, do you? All I can say is, I’d like to see him hanged.”

===Chinese===
「老天爷啊！」老人叫道，「竟有这样的事！好惨的一桩案子。这么说，你们认为已经拿住凶手了？我只想说一句：我巴不得亲眼看他上绞架。」

===Original===
“It seems clear from various things,” Tanner went on, “that the trouble originated before Douglas went to America. Now Edward Dale knew Sir William in those days. That is coincidence Number Two.”

===Chinese===
「从种种迹象看，」坦纳接着说，「祸根是在道格拉斯去美国之前种下的。而爱德华·戴尔当年就认识威廉爵士。这是巧合之二。」

===Original===
“You said, I think, that Douglas’s history could not be traced before he became a clerk in the Pennsylvania Railway? How then do you know he left England prior to that?”

===Chinese===
「我记得您说过，道格拉斯在宾夕法尼亚铁路当文书之前的经历无从查考？那你们又怎么知道他是在那之前离开英国的呢？」

===Original===
“We don’t absolutely know, but we think it for two reasons: first, he can speak with a North of England accent, and secondly, that in an old book of his we found the photograph of the Dales’ grave.”

===Chinese===
「我们并不能确知，只是有两点使我们如此推断：第一，他说话带英格兰北部口音；第二，在他的一本旧书里，我们发现了戴尔家坟墓的照片。」

===Original===
The other nodded.

===Chinese===
老人点了点头。

===Original===
“That photograph,” continued Tanner, “is coincidence Number Three. Few men would have such a photograph unless it represented something connected with their own families. And coincidence Number Four, Mr. Clayton, is this. Douglas is a very short man with very small hands and feet.”

===Chinese===
「那张照片，」坦纳继续道，「是巧合之三。除非拍的是与自家相关的坟茔，极少有人会存这样一张照片。至于巧合之四，克莱顿先生，是这个：道格拉斯身量很矮，手和脚都非常小。」

===Original===
“God bless my soul!” Mr. Clayton exclaimed again. “But this is most interesting. Go on, Mr. Tanner.”

===Chinese===
「老天爷啊！」克莱顿先生又叫了起来，「这可太有意思了。接着说，坦纳先生。」

===Original===
“Well, sir, that leads me up to a very obvious question. You must have guessed it. You have known Dale intimately in the past; could you identify him now?”

===Chinese===
「那么，先生，这就引出一个再明白不过的问题了，您必定已经猜到。您当年与戴尔相熟；如今还能把他认出来吗？」

===Original===
Tanner sat back in his chair and drew at his cigar. The other did not answer for a moment. Then as he slowly refilled his pipe, he said hesitatingly:

===Chinese===
坦纳靠回椅背，吸了口雪茄。老人沉吟了一会儿没有作答。随后，他缓缓往烟斗里装着烟丝，迟疑地说：

===Original===
“I hardly like to say. Thirty-eight years is a long time, and a man might change a lot during it. I think I would recognise Edward if I saw him, but I couldn’t be sure.”

===Chinese===
「这我可不敢说死。三十八年不是短日子，人在其间能变去许多。我想，若见到爱德华，我认得出，可不敢打包票。」

===Original===
“Then, sir, my second question follows naturally. Will you come up to London and try?”

===Chinese===
「那么，先生，我的第二个问题就顺理成章了。您可愿上伦敦来试一试？」

===Original===
The other smiled.

===Chinese===
老人笑了。

===Original===
“It’s a long journey for a man of my years,” he said, “but I imagine I have no choice. You Scotland Yard people are so autocratic.”

===Chinese===
「对我这把年纪的人来说，这路可够远的，」他说，「不过我想我也没得挑。你们苏格兰场的人就是这般说一不二。」

===Original===
Tanner smiled in his turn.

===Chinese===
坦纳也笑了。

===Original===
“If you will come at our expense, sir, you will confer a great favour on us. Do you prefer day or night travel?”

===Chinese===
「先生，若蒙您屈驾前来，川资全由我们承担，那真是帮了大忙。您喜欢白天赶路还是夜间赶路？」

===Original===
“Day. When would you like me to go?”

===Chinese===
「白天。你们要我几时动身？」

===Original===
“The sooner the better, sir. Tomorrow, if it would be convenient.”

===Chinese===
「越快越好，先生。方便的话，明天就成。」

===Original===
“One day is much like another to me. I will go tomorrow, if you like.”

===Chinese===
「对我来说，哪天都一样。你们愿意，我明天就走。」

===Original===
They sat on for some time longer smoking and chatting. In spite of his years Mr. Clayton’s mind remained active and vigorous, and he had kept himself well abreast of recent events. He evidently enjoyed exchanging ideas with the Inspector, and the latter exerted himself to entertain the old gentleman, relating several of the adventures he had met with in his professional career.

===Chinese===
两人又坐着吸了半晌烟，谈了半晌话。克莱顿先生虽已高龄，头脑依旧活跃矫健，于时下大事也不隔膜。他显然乐于同探长交换见解，探长也打起精神款待这位老先生，讲了自己办案生涯中亲历的几桩奇遇。

===Original===
In the afternoon Tanner called at the Eagle Works. But there he got no help. The firm’s official records did not go back far enough to include the Dales’ names, and none of the office staff recalled the brothers’ affairs.

===Chinese===
下午，坦纳到「雄鹰」炼铁厂走了一趟，却一无所获。商行的正式档案上溯不到那么久，载不进戴尔兄弟的名字；职员中也没有人记得这兄弟俩的事。

===Original===
On the following day the Inspector and Mr. Clayton travelled up to town together, and the former saw his new friend to an hotel. The interview with Douglas was to take place next morning.

===Chinese===
次日，探长与克莱顿先生一同乘车进伦敦，前者把新交的老朋友送到一家旅馆安顿下来。与道格拉斯的对质，定于翌日上午举行。

===Original===
Inspector Tanner delighted in a dramatic situation, especially when he was the deus ex machina. In the present instance he thought he was sufficiently sure of his ground to risk an audience. After consulting his chief, he accordingly rang up James Daunt.

===Chinese===
坦纳探长素来喜爱戏剧性的场面，尤其是当那扭转乾坤的角色由他本人担纲的时候。眼下的局面，他自觉已有足够把握，值得冒险请几位观客。于是他先请示了上司，随后给詹姆斯·多恩特挂了电话。

===Original===
“I think I am on to a clue at last,” he said. “As you and Miss Drew are interested and have helped so much I will stretch a point from strict etiquette and invite you both to be present while we test it tomorrow.﻿ ﻿… Yes, here at the Yard at eleven o’clock.”

===Chinese===
「我想我终于摸到线索了，」他说，「既然您和德鲁小姐都关切此案，又帮了这么多的忙，我就破一次例，不拘正规礼数，请二位明天到场，亲眼看看我们如何验证。……好，就在苏格兰场，十一点钟。」

===Original===
At the time appointed a little group sat in the Inspector’s room. There was first of all Miss Drew, dressed quietly in a navy blue coat and skirt, and a small hat. Her kindly, dependable face was pale and somewhat drawn, as if the strain of the last few weeks had taken its toll of her. But she was calm and pleasantly courteous as usual, and did not betray by word or deed the anxiety which was gnawing at her heart.

===Chinese===
到了约定时刻，一小群人在探长办公室里坐定。头一位是德鲁小姐，一身素净的藏青上衣配裙子，头上一顶小帽。她那张和善可靠的面孔有些苍白清减，仿佛近几周的煎熬已在她身上收了利息。但她一如既往地沉静、周到客气，言谈举止之间，丝毫没有流露那啮咬着内心的焦虑。

===Original===
Jimmy Daunt, who sat beside her, seemed the more nervous of the two. He was extremely dissatisfied at the way his case was going, and eagerly anxious to learn in what direction the Inspector’s fresh information would tend.

===Chinese===
坐在她身旁的吉米·多恩特，看来是两人中更紧张的那一个。他对案子的走向极为不满，急切地想知道探长的新线索究竟指向何方。

===Original===
Mr. Clayton, who sat next to Daunt, was anxious too. He devoutly hoped that after all the fuss and trouble of his visit to London, he should be able to give a decided opinion﻿—to say definitely whether the man he was to see was or was not Dale.

===Chinese===
坐在多恩特身旁的克莱顿先生，心里也不塌实。他虔诚地盼望，这趟劳师动众的伦敦之行，到头来自己能给出一个明确的意见——断然说出将见到的那人究竟是不是戴尔。

===Original===
On the other side of Tanner sat Chief Inspector Edgar. On Tanner reporting what he proposed to do, the latter had expressed a desire to be present. He it was who had suggested having the meeting at the Yard, in order to avoid the necessity of Miss Drew’s visiting the prison. But he took no part in the proceedings, Tanner conducting all the business.

===Chinese===
坦纳另一侧坐着埃德加总探长。坦纳汇报了自己的打算之后，总探长表示愿意到场。正是他提议把会面设在苏格兰场，免得德鲁小姐非去监狱走一遭。但事情全由坦纳主持，他并不插手。

===Original===
When the visitors had been introduced to each other, Tanner rose, and bowing to Miss Drew and her cousin, said:

===Chinese===
来客彼此引见过后，坦纳起身，向德鲁小姐和她表哥欠了欠身，说道：

===Original===
“I have taken the liberty of asking you to be present this morning, as I know the keen interest you take in this case. Following a certain line of inquiry, with the details of which I need not now trouble you, I had the good fortune to come across Mr. Clayton here. From what he told me there seemed a reasonable probability that the man whom I arrested in Portugal, and who gave his name as William Douglas, was not so named at all, but was a certain Edward Dale, a clerk in the late Sir William Ponson’s Ironworks, who emigrated to the States in the year 1882. Mr. Clayton has been good enough to come up all the way from Newcastle to put this theory to the test. I propose now to confront Douglas with Mr. Clayton, so as to see whether the two men recognise each other. I may add that if Douglas has to admit he is Dale, it is more than possible he may make a statement explaining the whole affair. Now, Mr. Clayton, might I ask you to sit here at my desk with your head bent as if writing, and when I sign to you, to move round so that Douglas may see your face suddenly.”

===Chinese===
「今天上午斗胆请二位到场，是因为我知道二位对此案何等关切。顺着某条线索查访——其中细节，现在不必烦扰各位——我有幸遇到了这位克莱顿先生。据他所言，颇有几分可能：我在葡萄牙逮捕的那名自称威廉·道格拉斯的男子，根本不姓这个姓，而是某个爱德华·戴尔，已故威廉·庞森爵士炼铁厂的文书，1882 年移居美国。克莱顿先生慨然从纽卡斯尔远道赶来，正是要验证这个推断。我现在打算让道格拉斯与克莱顿先生当面相对，看看两人是否彼此相识。我还可以补一句：倘若道格拉斯不得不承认自己就是戴尔，他很可能就会作出交代，把整件事情解释清楚。好了，克莱顿先生，可否请您坐到我书桌这儿，俯着头像写字一般，等我向您示意，再转过身来，好让道格拉斯冷不防看见您的脸。」

===Original===
A roll top desk was placed at right angles to the wall beside the large double window, and Mr. Clayton crossed over and sat down on the swing chair, bending forward as if to write. Anyone entering would see only his stooped shoulders, and the back of his head, but when he swung round his features would be fully lighted from the window. The others placed themselves with their backs to the light, and in view of the door. When he was satisfied as to the position of each, Tanner pressed a bell and a sergeant of the police entered.

===Chinese===
一张卷盖式书桌安放在大楼窗旁、与墙壁成直角的地方。克莱顿先生走过去，在转椅上坐下，俯身向前，作势欲写。进门的人只能看见他佝偻的双肩和后脑勺，而他一转身，窗光便会把他的面容照个通亮。其余诸人背光而坐，正对房门。坦纳见各人位置妥帖，便按了铃，一名警佐走了进来。

===Original===
“You may bring him in now.”

===Chinese===
「现在可以带他进来了。」

===Original===
The man withdrew, closing the door, and silence came down on the little group. To Lois Drew such scenes were new, and on her expressive features there was a look of compassion for the unhappy man for whom the trap was set, and whose life might depend on his actions during the next few moments. To her the whole business was evidently extremely distasteful, and it was not hard to conclude that only the possibility of helping her lover had induced her to continue to take part in it.

===Chinese===
那人退出房门，随手带上了门，一小群人顿时陷入沉默。对洛伊丝·德鲁来说，这样的场面是破天荒，她那富于表情的脸上，带着一抹怜悯，怜悯那个不幸的人——陷阱为他而设，而他的性命，也许就系于接下来几分钟的举止。这整套事情在她显然是万分难以接受的；不难断定，若非还想着救她的爱人，她断不会继续参与其中。

===Original===
Tanner’s emotions were evidently far otherwise. The eagerness of the hunter showed in his eyes, and his whole body seemed on the stretch. He was by no means a cruel man, but he had pitted his wits against the other, and the issue between them was now about to be joined.

===Chinese===
坦纳的心绪显然截然两样。他眼中露出猎手的渴望，浑身上下像绷紧了弦。他绝非残忍之人，但他已同对方斗上了智，两人的较量此刻便要见分晓。

===Original===
A knock came to the door, it was thrown open, and William Douglas entered.

===Chinese===
门上响起叩声，门随即被推开，威廉·道格拉斯走了进来。

===Original===
The man seemed to have aged since Tanner had first seen him at his house at Yelverton. His face was paler, his hair seemed greyer, and he was even smaller and more stooped. Innocent or guilty, he was already paying for his connection with the crime.

===Chinese===
自坦纳在耶尔弗顿他家中初见以来，这人仿佛又见老了。脸色更苍白，头发似乎更花白，身量愈发矮小佝偻。无论有罪无罪，他已经在这桩罪案的牵连中付出代价了。

===Original===
“Take a seat, Mr. Douglas,” said Tanner, moving forward and placing a chair where the full light from the window shone on the other’s face. “I have asked you to meet my friends here, to discuss some points about this case. But I have to repeat my warning that you are not bound to make any statement or to answer any questions you may be asked unless you choose. This lady is Miss Drew, a friend of the Ponson family; this gentleman,” he indicated Daunt, “is Mr. Austin Ponson’s solicitor, and this,” he waved his other hand, “is Chief Inspector Edgar. I think you already know our friend at the desk.”

===Chinese===
「请坐，道格拉斯先生，」坦纳说着，迎上前去，把一把椅子安在窗光正照着脸面的地方。「我请你来见见这里的几位朋友，商谈本案的几个要点。但我必须重申先前的警告：除非出于自愿，你没有义务作任何陈述，也没有义务回答人家问你的任何问题。这位是德鲁小姐，庞森家的朋友；这位先生，」他指了指多恩特，「是奥斯汀·庞森先生的律师；这一位，」他又扬了扬另一只手，「是埃德加总探长。我想，书桌那边那位朋友，你已经认识了。」

===Original===
As Tanner spoke he signed to Mr. Clayton, who swung round suddenly and faced Douglas.

===Chinese===
坦纳说话间向克莱顿先生使了个眼色，后者猛地转过身来，正对道格拉斯。

===Original===
The latter had seemed very much mystified by the whole proceedings. His eyes had followed Tanner’s gestures as each member of the party had been mentioned, and he had made each a slight bow. But when he saw Mr. Clayton’s face he remained as if turned to stone. At first for a moment he seemed puzzled and doubtful, then his eyes fixed themselves in a tense stare on the other’s features, his face grew slowly pale and drops of sweat formed on his forehead. Then, as if some second thought had passed through his mind, an expression of something like relief showed in his eyes. So he sat, staring, motionless.

===Chinese===
这整套安排，道格拉斯先前分明是满头雾水。每提到在场一人，他的目光便随着坦纳的手势转去，向每人微微颔首为礼。可当他看见克莱顿先生的脸时，整个人顿时僵住，如同化成了石头。起初一瞬，他似乎又疑又惑，随即双眼紧紧盯住对方的脸，面色渐渐转白，额头上沁出汗珠。接着，仿佛心中转过什么别的念头，他眼里露出近乎释然的神情。他就那样坐着，直勾勾地，一动不动。

===Original===
But if the effect of the meeting on Douglas was disconcerting, it was as nothing to that produced on Mr. Clayton. On first seeing the newcomer, he too looked puzzled and doubtful. Then gradually an expression of utter astonishment spread over his features. He literally gasped, and seemed so overwhelmed with amazement as to be bereft of the power of speech.

===Chinese===
如果说这场会面给道格拉斯的震动够大了，比起克莱顿先生所受的，却又算不得什么。初见进来的这人，他也是满脸疑疑惑惑。继而，一种彻骨的错愕渐渐布满他的面庞。他当真倒抽了一口气，惊得目瞪口呆，连说话的本领都失掉了。

===Original===
The surprise on the countenances of the two chief actors in the scene was reflected faintly on the faces of Lois Drew and the solicitor. But on Tanner’s there was triumph. If the girl and her cousin had not realised what was happening, he had. His plan had succeeded. That these two knew each other was established beyond any possibility of denial. It was as if each had shouted his recognition of the other aloud. He spoke quietly to the suspected man.

===Chinese===
场中两位主角脸上的惊愕，也淡淡映在洛伊丝·德鲁和律师的脸上。而坦纳脸上分明是胜利。姑娘和她表哥许是没有明白发生了什么，他却明白了。他的计划成功了。这两人彼此相识，已铁案如山，任谁也无法抵赖，就像两人都曾高声喊出认出对方一般。他平静地开口，对那名嫌疑男子说：

===Original===
“So you really are Edward Dale?”

===Chinese===
「这么说，你果真是爱德华·戴尔？」

===Original===
The words seemed to restore the power of movement to Mr. Clayton.

===Chinese===
这句话仿佛让克莱顿先生恢复了行动的能力。

===Original===
“No,” he almost shouted in his excitement. “It’s not Edward Dale. It’s Tom!”

===Chinese===
「不，」他激动得几乎喊出声来，「他不是爱德华·戴尔。他是汤姆！」

===Original===
Tanner jumped as if struck in the face.

===Chinese===
坦纳霍地一震，如同脸上挨了一记。

===Original===
“What?” he stammered. “What’s that you say? Tom? But﻿—but﻿—I thought﻿—”

===Chinese===
「什么？」他结结巴巴地说，「你说什么？汤姆？可——可——我还以为——」

===Original===
His voice trailed away into silence as the meaning of this discovery began to penetrate into his mind. Tom Dale was lost in the *Numidian* disaster thirty-five years before﻿—so he had been told, and so everyone had believed. But everyone must have been wrong. If this were Tom, he must have escaped from the wreck. He must have escaped and he must have concealed his escape. Why? Why should he conceal it? Why, to get rid of his wife, of course. It was a case of desertion. He had had all her money; he hated her. Of course that was it. He would take the opportunity to change his name and make a bid for freedom. But his wife﻿—And then Tanner gasped in his turn as he saw the further consequences involved. His wife had married Sir William Ponson, thinking her first husband was dead. But now it was clear that had been no marriage at all. Lady Ponson was Lady Ponson no longer, but Mrs. Tom Dale﻿—the wife of the drunken ex-clerk and suspected blackmailer! Sir William was not married. Austin and Enid were illegitimate! No wonder Sir William submitted to blackmail rather than allow such a scandal to become public. As innocent in the matter as the babes unborn, Sir William and the woman he had considered his wife, as well as his son and daughter, would have had to pay as dear as if the whole affair had been deliberate.

===Chinese===
随着这一发现的含义渐渐渗入脑海，他的声音低了下去，终至无声。汤姆·戴尔早在三十五年前就随「努米底亚人」号罹难了——他听来的是如此，人人相信的也是如此。可看来人人都弄错了。倘若这人是汤姆，那他必定是从沉船里逃了生。他必定逃了生，而且必定把逃生一事隐瞒了下来。为什么？他为什么要隐瞒？为什么——自然是为了甩掉他的妻子。这是一桩遗弃。她的钱已尽数落进他手里；他恨她。当然就是这么回事。他趁此机会改名换姓，博一个自由身。可他的妻子——想到这里，坦纳也不由倒抽一口冷气，因为他看清了此事牵出的更深远后果。他的妻子以为是死了前夫，才嫁给威廉·庞森爵士。可现在清楚了，那桩婚事根本不成立。庞森夫人再不是庞森夫人，而是汤姆·戴尔太太——那个酗酒的前文书、涉嫌敲诈的男人的妻子！威廉爵士并未成婚。奥斯汀和伊妮德都是私生！难怪威廉爵士宁可忍受敲诈，也不肯让这样一桩丑闻张扬出去。在这件事上，威廉爵士、被他视作妻子的女人，连同他的一子一女，都无辜得如同未出世的婴孩，却不得不付出惨重的代价，仿佛这整个事情是蓄意为之似的。

===Original===
Tanner glanced at Mr. Clayton. His excitement had subsided, and a look of fierce indignation against Dale was showing on his face. Tanner spoke.

===Chinese===
坦纳瞥了一眼克莱顿先生。老人的激动已经平复，脸上显出对戴尔切齿的愤慨。坦纳开口了。

===Original===
“I suppose there can be no mistake?”

===Chinese===
「我想，总不至于认错吧？」

===Original===
“Mistake?” the other burst out. “Man alive, look at him. By heaven I wish there was a mistake!”

===Chinese===
「认错？」老人脱口喊道，「老兄，你看看他。老天在上，我倒真希望是认错了！」

===Original===
“We had better bring him up to Gateshead, and see if anyone else will confirm your identification.”

===Chinese===
「我们最好还是带他回盖茨黑德，看看还有没有别人能印证您的指认。」

===Original===
There was an interruption from the prisoner.

===Chinese===
囚犯插了话。

===Original===
“You needn’t trouble,” he said sadly. “I admit it. I am Tom Dale.”

===Chinese===
「不必麻烦了，」他凄然说道，「我承认。我就是汤姆·戴尔。」

===Original===
“You escaped from the *Numidian*?”

===Chinese===
「你从『努米底亚人』号上逃生了？」

===Original===
“I escaped. I was picked up by a fishing smack and taken into Gloucester. I was on board four days before we got in, and I had plenty of time to make my plans. I don’t pretend I wasn’t wrong, but I wasn’t so bad as you think. I dare say you won’t believe me, but I did it for Ethel’s sake. She was tied to me, and I knew I was a bad egg and had all but ruined her. And what’s more, I knew I would ruin her outright if I went back to her. So I deserted her. But all I rid her of was trouble. I thought I would give her another chance with her life, and I did. I swore she would never know. And if I did go wrong, she at least has had her life happy since because of it.”

===Chinese===
「我是逃生了。一艘渔帆船把我救起，送进了格洛斯特（Gloucester）。在船上漂了四天才进港，我有的是工夫定主意。我不装作自己没错，可我也没有你们想的那么坏。你们多半不会相信，但我那样做是为了埃塞尔。她拴在我身上，而我知道自己是个坏蛋，险些把她毁了。还不止如此，我知道，我若回到她身边，就会把她毁得干干净净。所以我遗弃了她。可我给她除掉的，只是苦难。我想让她的人生再有一次机会，我也做到了。我发过誓，绝不让她知道。退一步说，就算我后来走了邪路，至少她此后的人生因之得了幸福。」

===Original===
The man spoke simply, and with a certain dignity which impressed his hearers.

===Chinese===
这人说得朴实，还带着几分尊严，令听者为之动容。

===Original===
“How did you conceal your identity?” Tanner asked.

===Chinese===
「你是怎么瞒住身份的？」坦纳问。

===Original===
“Very easily. I had made friends on the voyage with another passenger. He had told me he was alone in the world. I saw him drown. I took his name.”

===Chinese===
「轻而易举。航行途中，我同另一名乘客交上了朋友。他对我说过，他在这个世上孑然一身。我亲眼看着他淹死。我拿走了他的名字。」

===Original===
“And then you came here and blackmailed the man you had injured?”

===Chinese===
「然后你就回到这里，敲诈那个被你伤害过的人？」

===Original===
Dale nodded his head slowly.

===Chinese===
戴尔缓缓点了点头。

===Original===
“I admit that too,” he said sorrowfully. “I most bitterly regret it, but I must admit it. I do not want to make any excuses for that, but here again the facts are not quite so black as they look. When I had been out there about thirty years I got a longing for the old country. I had made a little money in the States, and I left my job and came over to England. I was afraid to go back to Gateshead, so I looked around and took that cottage in Devonshire. Then one day in London I met Ponson﻿—I didn’t know he had a handle to his name then. He recognised me, and there was a scene. I thought he would have killed me in the street. Then I got him into a bar, and we took a private room and had it out. I understood he had a right to have a down on me for deserting Ethel, but at first I couldn’t understand why he was so absolutely mad. Then I learnt. I hadn’t known what had happened to Ethel, for I was too much afraid of arousing suspicion as to who I was, to go back to Gateshead or make any inquiries. He threatened me so wildly I got afraid for my life, and then I saw how I could turn the tables on him. I told him that so far from me being in his power, he was in mine. I told him I would make the affair public myself, and that if I could be punished I would take it, and he could have the scandal. He blustered at first, then gradually he saw his position, and then he crawled. He offered to make the thing a business proposition. He would pay for my silence. He pressed his offer on me, and I accepted it at last. And I have at least kept my word. Not a whisper of the affair has passed my lips. But I admit taking the money. I was very hard up, and it meant a lot to me. You don’t understand, gentlemen, how much a few pounds means to a poor man. And with all his thousands he didn’t miss it. Not any more than you would miss a penny if you dropped it. I took it and I admit I pressed him for more.”

===Chinese===
「这个我也承认，」他悲声说道，「我悔之已极，但必须承认。这件事我不打算找任何托辞，不过同样，实情也不像表面看上去那样漆黑。我在那边待了大约三十年，起了思乡之念，想念故土。我在美国攒下一点钱，便辞了差事，渡海回了英格兰。我不敢回盖茨黑德，便四处物色，在德文郡赁下了那座小屋。后来有一天，我在伦敦遇上了庞森——那时我还不知道他名字前添了头衔。他认出了我，当场便闹了一场。我以为他要在大街上结果了我。后来我把他拉进一家酒馆，我们要了间雅座，把话摊开说尽。我明白，他为着我遗弃埃塞尔而恨我，是天经地义；可起初我实在不懂，他何以暴跳如雷到那个地步。后来我才得知。埃塞尔后来的遭际我并不知情，因为我太怕惹人疑心我的身份，既不敢回盖茨黑德，也不敢打听任何消息。他威胁得那样凶，我怕性命难保，随即看出自己可以反将一军。我告诉他，别说我在他的手心里，倒是他捏在我的手心里。我告诉他，这事我自己便会张扬出去，若治得了我的罪，我认了，丑闻就由他领受。起先他虚声恫吓，渐渐看清了自己的处境，然后就服软了。他提出把这事办成一桩交易：他出钱买我缄口。他把这提议死命往我身上塞，我到头来还是收下了。这一点上我至少守了约。此事我没吐露过半个字。但钱，我承认收了。我当时手头拮据，那钱对我不算小数。各位先生，你们不懂几镑钱于一个穷人意味着什么。而他家财万贯，这点钱他根本觉不出来，就像你掉了一枚便士也毫不心疼一样。我收了钱，而且我承认，还逼他多给过。」

===Original===
“Was that what you went to the Luce Manor boathouse for on the night of the murder?”

===Chinese===
「那么案发当晚，你去卢斯庄园船屋，就是为了这个？」

===Original===
Chief Inspector Edgar moved suddenly.

===Chinese===
埃德加总探长突然动了动。

===Original===
“Come, Tanner, that won’t do,” he advised, and then to Dale: “You needn’t answer that unless you like.”

===Chinese===
「得了，坦纳，这可不行，」他提醒道，又转向戴尔：「这个问题，你不愿答就不答。」

===Original===
Dale hesitated. To the others it seemed as if he was on the verge of a confession. Then he bowed to Edgar.

===Chinese===
戴尔迟疑了。在旁人看来，他似乎马上就要招认。随后他向埃德加欠了欠身。

===Original===
“Thank you,” he said. “I had perhaps better consult my solicitor first.”

===Chinese===
「多谢，」他说，「或许我还是先咨询一下我的律师为好。」

===Original===
Tanner looked annoyed, but he controlled himself and again addressed the prisoner.

===Chinese===
坦纳面有愠色，但他按捺住了，再次向囚犯开口。

===Original===
“Then you don’t wish to make any statement?”

===Chinese===
「这么说，你不愿作任何陈述了？」

===Original===
“No. Not at present, at any rate.”

===Chinese===
「不愿。至少眼下不愿。」

===Original===
Tanner nodded and rang his bell. The same sergeant reentered and signed to Dale to follow him. Then, including the whole party in a slight bow, the latter rose from his chair and the two men left the room.

===Chinese===
坦纳点点头，按了铃。还是那名警佐走进来，示意戴尔跟他走。戴尔向满座众人微微一躬，从椅子上起身，两人一同出了房门。

===Original===
The silence which fell when the door was closed lasted a full thirty seconds, and then Daunt broke it.

===Chinese===
门关上之后，屋里的沉默足足持续了三十秒钟，随后是多恩特开的口。

===Original===
“Well, Inspector, this has been very interesting, but I’m hanged if I can make head or tail of it. Maybe you’d explain to Miss Drew and me what it’s all about.”

===Chinese===
「唉，探长，这一出真是好看，可我委实摸不着半点头绪。你能不能给我和德鲁小姐讲讲，这到底是怎么回事？」

===Original===
Chief Inspector Edgar rose.

===Chinese===
埃德加总探长站起身来。

===Original===
“I think that’s all I’m concerned in,” he said, and turning to Mr. Clayton, he invited him to smoke a cigar in his room.

===Chinese===
「我想，与我有关的部分，到此为止了，」他说，随即转向克莱顿先生，请他去自己房里抽支雪茄。

===Original===
When Tanner was left alone with the cousins, he realised that he had an extremely unpleasant task to perform. Miss Drew, as the affianced wife of Austin Ponson, was personally interested in the story. Though from his knowledge of her he thought she would not place so great importance on the unfortunate occurrence as might a shallower and more conventional woman, yet the news could not be otherwise than a shock to her. He wished someone else had the telling of it.

===Chinese===
屋里只剩坦纳和这对表兄妹时，他意识到自己面前是一桩极不愉快的差事。德鲁小姐身为奥斯汀·庞森的未婚妻，这个故事与她切身相关。凭他对她的了解，他料想她不会像一个更浅薄、更拘于俗见的女人那样，把这件不幸之事看得天崩地裂；可这消息对她终究不啻一记打击。他倒宁愿把话出口的是旁的什么人。

===Original===
But no purpose would be saved by delay. The sooner he began, the sooner the unwelcome job would be over.

===Chinese===
可拖延无济于事。越是早开口，这桩不受欢迎的差事便越早了结。

===Original===
Asking Miss Drew’s permission, he passed his cigar case to Daunt, then drew forward his chair and began to speak.

===Chinese===
征得德鲁小姐首肯，他把雪茄匣递给多恩特，然后把椅子往前挪了挪，开口说话。

===Original===
“I am afraid the story I have to tell you will come rather as a shock to you,” he said, as he examined the end of his cigar. “This man Dale had been blackmailing Sir William Ponson for the last four years. There was an exceedingly unfortunate secret in Sir William’s life﻿—unknown to him until he learnt it from Dale at that time, four years ago. Sir William was in no wise to blame for what had taken place. So far as I can learn, he had acted with scrupulous honour all through. The fault was Dale’s and Dale’s only. But to make it clear I must tell you from the beginning.”

===Chinese===
「恐怕我要讲的事，二位听了会相当震惊，」他端详着雪茄的烟头说。「这个戴尔，过去四年一直在敲诈威廉·庞森爵士。威廉爵士的生活里藏着一个极为不幸的秘密——直到四年前戴尔向他摊牌，他才知道有这回事。已发生的一切，威廉爵士毫无过错。就我所能查知的，他自始至终行事光明磊落。错在戴尔，且只在戴尔一人。不过要说清楚，我得从头讲起。」

===Original===
The cousins remained almost motionless while the Inspector related the details of his journey north, and the facts he had there learnt; the family history of the Dales; how the brothers came across Sir William in the Eagle Ironworks; the rivalry between Ponson and Tom Dale for the hand of the pretty Ethel Osborne; Tom’s success in the contest; the unhappy married life of the young couple; Tom’s mission to Canada; the loss of the *Numidian*, and his presumed death; Sir William’s marriage with Mrs. Dale, and the birth of Austin and Enid; Tom Dale’s return to England; and finally the blackmailing, culminating in the presumed negotiations for the purchase of the annuity.

===Chinese===
探长细讲他北上的经过和在当地查得的种种事实时，这对表兄妹几乎一动不动：戴尔家的家史；兄弟俩如何在「雄鹰」炼铁厂与威廉爵士相识；庞森与汤姆·戴尔争求俊俏的埃塞尔·奥斯本之手；汤姆如何在竞争中得手；年轻夫妇不幸的婚姻生活；汤姆的加拿大差事；「努米底亚人」号的失事和他推定的死讯；威廉爵士迎娶戴尔太太，奥斯汀与伊妮德相继出世；汤姆·戴尔回到英格兰；最后是那桩敲诈，一直演到推断中的年金交易谈判为止。

===Original===
“But the murder?” asked Daunt when at last the Inspector paused. “What about that?”

===Chinese===
「可是谋杀案呢？」探长终于住口时，多恩特问道，「那怎么说？」

===Original===
“The murder unfortunately remains as great a mystery as ever,” Tanner answered slowly; “indeed, even more so,” and he explained his difficulty about, as he expressed it, the wrong man having been killed.

===Chinese===
「不幸得很，谋杀案仍旧和从前一样是个谜，」坦纳缓缓答道，「非但如此，谜团反倒更重了。」接着他说明了自己的难处——用他的话说，是死错了人。

===Original===
The two men discussed the affair for some minutes further, Miss Drew remaining silent. When they got up to take their leave her eyes were very bright, and there was an expression on her face the Inspector could not quite fathom.

===Chinese===
两人又就案情商讨了几分钟，德鲁小姐始终沉默。起身告辞时，她双眼异常明亮，脸上带着一种探长捉摸不透的神情。

===Original===
“Mr. Tanner,” she said as she held out her hand, “I would like to thank you for the way you told your story.”

===Chinese===
「坦纳先生，」她伸出手来，「我想谢谢您——谢谢您讲这个故事时的那番用心。」

===Original===
Outside the Yard she dismissed Daunt.

===Chinese===
出了苏格兰场，她打发走了多恩特。

===Original===
“I am going to write to Austin,” she announced. “I shall leave the letter at your office shortly after lunch, then, like a dear man, you will take it to him immediately, and bring me the answer.”

===Chinese===
「我要给奥斯汀写信，」她说，「午饭后不久，我把信送到你办公室，然后呢，劳你这大好人，立刻替我送去给他，再把回信带回来。」

===Original===
“Of course, I will, old girl,” Daunt answered her as they parted.

===Chinese===
「当然照办，好姑娘，」两人分手时，多恩特如此作答。

## XV · In the Luce Manor Boathouse / XV · 卢斯庄园船屋中

===Original===
That same evening Daunt paid his promised visit to Austin Ponson. He found his client seated despondently in his cell, his head resting on his hands. Like Dale, he had aged since his arrest. His face was pale and drawn, his eyes weary, and as he moved his head a suspicion of grey showed at his temples. His manner had lost its old ease and lightness, and it was evident that the crisis through which he was passing would leave its mark on him for many a day to come.

===Chinese===
当晚，多恩特如约去探视奥斯汀·庞森。他见当事人正颓然坐在牢房里，双手托着头。和戴尔一样，自被捕以来，他也见老了。他脸色苍白憔悴，眼中满是倦意，脑袋一动，鬓角便隐约露出一丝灰白。往日那种从容轻捷的气度已然不见。显然，他正在经历的这场劫难，日后很长一段日子都会在他身上留下印记。

===Original===
“I have something for you, Mr. Ponson,” the solicitor said as he sat down and felt in his pocket for Lois’s letter. “You are to let me have an answer.”

===Chinese===
「我给您带来一样东西，庞森先生，」这位律师坐下身，伸手到口袋里去摸洛伊丝的信，一面说道，「您读完，得给我个回音。」

===Original===
Austin’s eyes lit up as he saw the handwriting, and he seized the note with eagerness. To let him read it in peace, Daunt drew some papers from his pocket and began to study them. But he hadn’t done so for many seconds when an exclamation drew his attention back to the other.

===Chinese===
奥斯汀一见那笔迹，眼睛顿时亮了，迫不及待地一把抓过信去。为了让他安心读信，多恩特从口袋里掏出几份文件，自顾自地看了起来。可没看几秒钟，一声惊呼就把他的注意力又引回到对方身上。

===Original===
Austin Ponson had risen to his feet and was excitedly pacing the cell. He was a transformed man. A smile was on his lips, his eyes were shining, and his face had a rapt and beatific expression, like that of a man who sees a vision of angels.

===Chinese===
奥斯汀·庞森已经站起身来，兴奋地在牢房里来回踱步。他简直换了一个人：嘴角挂着笑，双眼放光，脸上是一种恍如得见天使显圣般的狂喜而蒙福的神情。

===Original===
“My Heavens!” he cried, “*What* a girl! She’s beyond anything I could have imagined. One in a thousand millions! I can hardly realise it. I tell you, Daunt, if I never get out of this hell again, it’s been worth it. It would be worth any suffering to get such a letter. Tell her﻿—But you can’t tell her. Nor I. No one could ever tell her what I feel.”

===Chinese===
「我的老天！」他叫道，「天底下竟有这样的姑娘！好得超出我一切的想象。十亿人里也挑不出第二个！我简直不敢相信是真的。我告诉你，多恩特，就算我再出不了这个地狱，也值了。能收到这样一封信，吃什么苦都值。告诉她——可你没法告诉她。我也没法。谁也没法把我此刻心里的感受告诉她。」

===Original===
He paused and looked at the other, then resumed his hurried pacing.

===Chinese===
他顿住，望了望对方，又急匆匆地踱起步来。

===Original===
“I swear that if I get out of this place, I’ll make it up to her. I’ll live for her day and night, and for nothing else. She’ll never regret what she has done﻿—that is,” he sank into his chair and the dejected look returned to his face, “if I ever do get out.”

===Chinese===
「我发誓，只要能离开这个地方，我一定加倍补偿她。我要日日夜夜只为她一个人活着，别的什么都不为。她绝不会为自己做过的事后悔——那就是，」他跌坐回椅子里，脸上重新罩上沮丧的神色，「如果我真出得去的话。」

===Original===
“You forget, Mr. Ponson, that I don’t know what’s in your note.”

===Chinese===
「您倒忘了，庞森先生，我还不知道您那封信里写的是什么哩。」

===Original===
Austin stared.

===Chinese===
奥斯汀一怔。

===Original===
“You don’t?” he queried in surprise. “Why, she tells me the whole thing’s out﻿—that Tom Dale has been found, and that she knows about my father’s marriage and my birth. And”﻿—his face lit up and he spoke triumphantly﻿—“she says she doesn’t mind﻿—that it will make no difference to”﻿—he paused as if for a word, then concluded﻿—“her feelings towards me. What do you think of that?”

===Chinese===
「您不知道？」他愕然问道，「嗐，她告诉我，事情全都摊开了——说汤姆·戴尔找到了，还说她已知道我父亲的婚事和我的身世。而且」——他容光焕发，得意地说下去——「她说她不介意——说这不会有任何改变」——他顿了顿，像在找一个词，然后补完——「她对我的感情。您说这事怎么样？」

===Original===
“I congratulate you very heartily, Mr. Ponson,” Daunt replied, though with a mental reservation. “But I can assure you that so far as I am aware, the whole thing is anything but ‘out.’ It is true the identity of William Douglas with Tom Dale has been discovered, and the effect his existence has on the validity of your father’s marriage is known. But that is all. No explanation of the murder has yet come to light. And, after all, that is really what matters.”

===Chinese===
「我衷心祝贺您，庞森先生，」多恩特答道，心里却不免打个问号，「不过我可以向您保证，据我所知，事情远远说不上『全都摊开了』。威廉·道格拉斯与汤姆·戴尔实系一人，这一身份业已查明；他既在世，对令尊那桩婚姻的效力有何影响，也已是人所共知。可也就到此为止。凶案至今没有半点解释。说到底，这才是真正要紧的事。」

===Original===
“Has Dale admitted his identity?”

===Chinese===
「戴尔承认自己的身份了吗？」

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
「承认了。」

===Original===
“And has he made no statement about what took place in the boathouse?”

===Chinese===
「那关于船屋里发生的事，他没有作任何交代？」

===Original===
“None. But, Mr. Ponson, that remark implies to me that you were there yourself and know.”

===Chinese===
「没有。不过，庞森先生，您这句话在我看来意味着：您本人当时在场，而且知情。」

===Original===
Austin looked sharply at the speaker.

===Chinese===
奥斯汀锐利地瞥了说话人一眼。

===Original===
“I didn’t say so,” he answered dryly, “but﻿—stop, let me think a moment.”

===Chinese===
「我没这么说，」他干巴巴地答道，「不过——慢着，让我想一会儿。」

===Original===
For some minutes silence reigned in the gloomy cell. To Daunt it had seemed as if his client was on the verge of a confession, and he wondered if one more sordid story was to be added to the list of those to which the grey walls of this grim apartment had in all probability listened. Austin sat motionless, his mind evidently engrossed with some problem, the solution of which eluded him. But at last he seemed to find it. Straightening himself up, he faced the solicitor.

===Chinese===
一连好几分钟，阴沉的牢房里寂然无声。在多恩特看来，当事人眼看就要招认了；他暗自思忖，这四壁灰墙的阴森斗室，多半已经听过不少见不得人的故事，莫非还要再添上一桩？奥斯汀一动不动地坐着，心思显然被某个难题整个占住了，只是寻不出解答。但他终于像是找到了。他挺直身子，面向律师。

===Original===
“This news you have brought makes a tremendous difference to me,” he said. “From my point of view there is no longer any reason why the events of that tragic night should not be known. I have remained silent for two reasons. First, because of my mother. The thought of her learning that she was still the wife of that drunken scoundrel was more than I could bear. You’ll understand my feelings﻿—the whole thing is so painful I hate to speak of it. Then there was another reason. I made the unforgivable blunder of being afraid to tell Miss Drew. I have paid for that already, and every bit of that payment I deserve ten times over. I distrusted her. I thought if the circumstances of my birth came out she might have nothing more to do with me. And I just couldn’t risk that. You see, it was not as if there had been any deliberate evil on my parents’ part. Both were utterly innocent, and even ignorant that anything was wrong. Therefore I could not see that I was called upon to chance the wrecking of my happiness on what was after all a mere technical matter only. God forgive me, I did not intend to tell that angel. I feared the stigma would remain. Well, I have suffered for it. As I ought to have known, she was above a petty feeling of that sort. I should have told her the moment I knew of the matter myself. I should have told Tanner everything at the start. Much trouble would have been saved. And now it may be too late. I may not be believed.”

===Chinese===
「您带来的这个消息，对我事关重大，」他说，「依我看，如今再没有理由不让那个悲惨之夜的经过为人所知。我之所以缄口，原因有二。第一，是为了我母亲。一想到她将知道自己仍然是那个醉鬼无赖的妻子，我就承受不住。您会理解我的心情——这整件事太叫人痛心，我连讲都不愿讲。此外还有一层原因。我犯了一个不可原谅的错误：我不敢把实情告诉德鲁小姐。这个错我已经付了代价，而且我受的每一点苦都还轻了，实打实该受上十倍。我当时不信任她。我心想，倘若我出身的情形传扬出去，她也许就再不跟我来往了。这个险我实在冒不起。您想，这又不是我父母存心作恶。他们两位全然无辜，甚至根本不知道事情有什么不妥。所以我想不明白，凭什么要我为了一桩说到底只算手续问题的区区小事，拿自己一生的幸福去撞运气。上帝饶恕我，我原是打算把这件事瞒着那位天使的。我怕污名洗不掉。唉，我到底为此吃了苦头。其实我早该如何道，她岂是那种小心眼的人。我自己一得知这件事，就该立刻告诉她；一开始就该把一切原原本本告诉坦纳。那样能省去多少麻烦。可如今也许太迟了。人家也许不肯相信我。」

===Original===
“I don’t know, of course, what you propose to tell,” said Daunt as the other paused, “but as your legal adviser I should warn you to be very careful of what you say to anyone. If you care to tell your story to me, I shall be pleased to advise you as to what, if any of it, should be made public.”

===Chinese===
「我自然无从知道您打算讲什么，」见对方住了口，多恩特说道，「不过，作为您的法律顾问，我应当提醒您：对任何人开口，都要千万谨慎。您若愿意把事情讲给我听，我乐于为您参详，其中哪些内容——如果确有需要公开的——应当公开。」

===Original===
“I suppose that would be wisest,” Austin returned, “but I have quite made up my mind. From the first I decided to tell all I knew if the secret of my parentage came out. That was the only reason for my silence. Indeed, I was coming to the conclusion I must tell in any case, even if I myself had to reveal it. I appreciate your offer and under other circumstances would gladly avail myself of your advice, but whatever the consequences, I am going to tell. But I by no means wish the affair to be made public. I want most of all to tell Miss Drew, I would like to tell you, and I must tell Tanner. If you will help me by making this possible, I will be forever your debtor. One other thing, I should like Cosgrove to know my decision. It is only due to him. Can you arrange these matters?”

===Chinese===
「那样办想必最稳妥，」奥斯汀答道，「不过我主意已经拿定。打一开始我就决定：一旦我身世的秘密败露，我就把知道的一切全部说出来。我保持缄默，唯一的原因就在这里。说实在的，我本来也已渐渐觉得，无论如何都得说，哪怕得由我亲口把这层窗户纸捅破。您的心意我领了，换在别的情形，我巴不得讨您的指教；可现在无论什么后果，我都非说不可了。不过我绝不希望这件事闹得满城风雨。我最要紧的是告诉德鲁小姐，也愿意告诉您，还必须告诉坦纳。您若肯促成这几桩事，我一辈子感激不尽。另外还有一件事：我想让科斯格罗夫知道我的决定。于他，这才算公道。这几件事您能安排吗？」

===Original===
“I think so. But I cannot but feel you may be making a mistake in not first taking, I don’t say my advice, but the advice of some legal man. I cannot move you on that point?”

===Chinese===
「我想可以。不过我总觉得，您没有先听取——倒不一定是我，而是某位法律界人士——的意见，恐怕是失策。这一点上，我说不动您么？」

===Original===
“My dear fellow,” said Austin warmly, “there is no one whose advice I would take more readily than yours. But in this case we need not discuss it. If you will not help me to the interview I want, I will send for Tanner and tell him here.”

===Chinese===
「亲爱的伙计，」奥斯汀诚恳地说，「天下人的话，我最乐于听的就是您的。可这件事，咱们就不必讨论了。您若不肯帮我安排这场会面，我就叫人去请坦纳，就在这儿说给他听。」

===Original===
Daunt saw there was no more to be said. He waited till Austin had written his reply to Lois﻿—a lengthy and complicated proceeding﻿—then, promising he would see Tanner without delay, he left the cell.

===Chinese===
多恩特见话已说尽，便等奥斯汀给洛伊丝写好回信——那是桩又长又费事的过程——然后答应毫不耽搁去见坦纳，便离开牢房去了。

===Original===
Inspector Tanner was keenly interested by the news.

===Chinese===
坦纳探长听到这个消息，大感兴趣。

===Original===
“At last!” he exclaimed in a satisfied tone. “We shall get the truth this time. He’s getting frightened. He’ll not bluff us any more.”

===Chinese===
「总算等到这天了！」他心满意足地呼道，「这一回我们可要听到实话了。他怕了。他再唬不了我们了。」

===Original===
“I don’t see that you’ve any right to say that,” Daunt returned hotly. “It will be time enough to accuse him of lying when you hear what he has to say.”

===Chinese===
「我看不出您有什么权利这么说，」多恩特火了，「等您听完了他要讲的话，再来指摘他说谎也不迟。」

===Original===
Tanner smiled.

===Chinese===
坦纳莞尔。

===Original===
“Very good, Mr. Daunt. I’ll not say a word﻿—till then.”

===Chinese===
「很好，多恩特先生。我一个字不说——到那时候再说。」

===Original===
It was arranged that the proceedings of that morning should be repeated next day. Austin would be brought to Scotland Yard, and there in the presence of Lois, Daunt, Tanner, and a stenographer, he could make his statement.

===Chinese===
于是安排定当：那天早晨的那套程序，第二天再照样来一遍——奥斯汀将被带到苏格兰场，在洛伊丝、多恩特、坦纳和一名速记员在场的情况下，作出他的陈述。

===Original===
At eleven o’clock next day Lois and Jimmy Daunt drove up to the Yard, and were shown without delay to Tanner’s room. There they met the Inspector and his Chief, Mr. Edgar, who had expressed a wish to be present on this occasion also. At the desk was a shorthand writer.

===Chinese===
第二天上午十一点，洛伊丝和吉米·多恩特驱车来到苏格兰场，随即被引到坦纳的房间。探长与他的上司埃德加总探长已在座——这位总探长也表示愿再度列席。桌前坐着一名速记员。

===Original===
It was cool in the grey walled room. The open window allowed a current of fresh air to flow gently in, carrying with it the subdued hum of the great city without. In the sunny courtyard the sparrows were twittering angrily, while a bluebottle buzzed endlessly up and down the window pane. The little group, after the first brief greetings, sat silent. Expectancy showed on every face, but whereas Tanner’s and the Chief Inspector’s also indicated satisfaction, uneasiness was marked on Daunt’s and positive apprehension on Lois’s. To her at least, the coming meeting with her lover was obviously no light ordeal. On it, as was evident to them all, largely depended the future happiness of both.

===Chinese===
四壁灰墙的房间里很凉爽。敞开的窗子放进一股新鲜空气，轻轻流入，裹挟着外面大都会低低的嗡鸣。洒满阳光的天井里，麻雀叽叽喳喳吵得正凶；一只绿头苍蝇在窗玻璃上没完没了地嗡嗡打转。这一小群人略一寒暄，便都默然坐定。人人脸上都写着期待，只是坦纳和总探长的神色里另有一层满意，多恩特脸上是不安，洛伊丝脸上更简直是忧惧。至少对她来说，与恋人的这场会面，显见得绝不是轻松一关。他们谁都看得明白：两人的幸福，大半就系在这上头。

===Original===
They had not waited long before a knock came to the door and a sergeant of police admitted Austin Ponson. The young man was dressed in a suit of navy blue, and bore himself quietly and with some dignity. In the bright light of the room the lines of suffering showed more clearly on his face, and his eyes looked still more weary.

===Chinese===
没等多久，门上一敲，一名警佐把奥斯汀·庞森领了进来。年轻人穿一身藏青西服，举止安静，颇见庄重。房间里光线明亮，把苦难在他脸上刻下的纹路照得愈发分明，那双眼睛也显得愈加疲惫。

===Original===
Instantly on entering they swept over those present, fixing themselves immediately on Lois. In spite of an evident effort for self control, the light of an absolute adoration shone in them for a moment, then he withdrew them, bowed generally to the company, and sat down.

===Chinese===
一进门，他的目光便扫过在场诸人，旋即定在洛伊丝身上。尽管明显在竭力自持，那一瞬间，他眼中仍闪出一腔毫无保留的深爱；随即他把目光收回来，向众人欠身致意，坐下了。

===Original===
But this was not enough for Lois. She sprang to her feet, and going over to him, held out her hand. He rose and clasped it, and though neither would trust themselves to speak, they saw that in each other’s eyes which satisfied them.

===Chinese===
可洛伊丝不以此为足。她霍地站起，走到他跟前，伸出手去。他也起身握住。两人谁都不敢开口，但彼此眼里都看见了那让各自心安的东西。

===Original===
Tanner with some delicacy busied himself for a few moments in giving directions to the stenographer, then turning to the others, he spoke.

===Chinese===
坦纳颇识轻重，先花了几分钟给速记员交代事项，然后转向众人，开了口。

===Original===
“I don’t think it is necessary, Miss Drew and gentlemen, for me to explain our presence here. Last night Mr. Daunt intimated to me that Mr. Ponson had a communication to make, at which he wished Miss Drew, Mr. Daunt, and myself to be present. This meeting has therefore been arranged. I have only to make known to you, Mr. Ponson, Chief Inspector Edgar”﻿—he indicated his colleague﻿—“and to ask you to proceed with your statement. It is, of course, understood by you that you make it voluntarily and that it may be used against you?”

===Chinese===
「德鲁小姐，各位先生，我想无须由我来解释我们为何在此。昨晚，多恩特先生告知我，庞森先生有一事要陈述，希望德鲁小姐、多恩特先生和我在场听他讲。因此安排了这次会面。我只需向您介绍，庞森先生——这位是埃德加总探长，」他指了指自己的同事，「然后就请您开始陈述。您当然明白：您是自愿作此陈述，而且它可能被用来反对您，对吗？」

===Original===
“I understand that right enough, Inspector,” began Austin, “and I wish to say I have no quarrel with your treatment of me. You have been fair all through.”

===Chinese===
「这一点我清楚得很，探长，」奥斯汀开口道，「而且我要说，你们对我毫无苛待，我并无怨言。你们自始至终都公道。」

===Original===
He paused, settled himself more comfortably in his chair, and went on:

===Chinese===
他顿了顿，在椅子里坐得更舒服些，接着讲道：

===Original===
“The only thing I should like to ask is whether my cousin, Cosgrove, has been told that I am going to make this statement?”

===Chinese===
「我唯一想问的是：我表哥科斯格罗夫可已得知我要作这份陈述？」

===Original===
“He has been told,” Tanner answered.

===Chinese===
「已告知他了。」坦纳答道。

===Original===
“And may I learn if he was satisfied?”

===Chinese===
「那我可不可以知道，他听了是否满意？」

===Original===
“He seemed so.”

===Chinese===
「看样子是满意的。」

===Original===
“Thank you. I am not quite sure how much of my story you know, but I shall tell you everything. When I have finished I shall have a request to make of you﻿—that you will keep what I am about to tell private﻿—but I do not know whether or not you will find that possible.”

===Chinese===
「谢谢。你们已经知道我这段经历中的多少，我不大有数，不过我会把一切和盘托出。讲完之后，我要向诸位提一个请求——请把我将要讲的内容保守秘密——只是我不知道你们做得到做不到。」

===Original===
Tanner nodded without speaking.

===Chinese===
坦纳点了点头，没有作声。

===Original===
“Of my early life,” went on Austin, “I do not think I need say much. I expect”﻿—he looked at Tanner﻿—“you know all about it. You know that, while we never had an open breach, my father and I did not pull well together. We looked at things from such different points of view that our intercourse only produced irritation. My father wished me to read for the bar with the idea of entering Parliament, and trying for a seat in the Ministry. I was not ambitious in that direction, but preferred literary work, and scientific research. Therefore, as you no doubt are aware, I found it irksome at home, and I set up my own establishment in Halford. But that we remained good friends was proved by my father’s moving to Luce Manor at my suggestion. With my mother I was always in sympathy. She was easygoing, and deferred without protest to my father’s decisions, but never at any time was there the slightest cloud between us. So things had gone on for years, and so they went on until this terrible business began.”

===Chinese===
「我的早年，」奥斯汀接着说，「我想不必多讲。我料想，」他朝坦纳看了一眼，「你们已经一清二楚。你们知道，我和父亲虽从未公开决裂，却始终合不来。我们看事情的角度相差太远，一交谈就只剩彼此怄气。父亲希望我攻读律师资格，日后进议会，再谋个内阁席位。我于那条路并无野心，偏爱文学写作和科学研究。因此——这一点诸位想必也知道——我在家里住得憋闷，便在哈尔福德自立了门户。但我和父亲始终是好朋友，这一点，由他听我的提议迁居卢斯庄园便足资证明。至于母亲，我们娘儿俩向来心意相通。她性子随和，父亲的决定她从不抗议、一概顺从，但我们母子之间从没有起过一丝云翳。多年来的日子便是这么过的，直到这场可怕的祸事开了头。」

===Original===
Austin moved nervously in his chair, glancing quickly round the little group.

===Chinese===
奥斯汀在椅子里局促地动了动，飞快地环视了一圈这一小群人。

===Original===
“On Sunday, 4th July,” he resumed, “occurred the first event of this unhappy tragedy, so far as I was concerned. I received by that morning’s post a letter from my father, saying he wished to see me on very private business, and asking me to dine and spend that evening with him. He directed me to destroy his letter, and not refer to the matter to anyone.

===Chinese===
「七月四日，星期日，」他继续说，「就我而论，这场不幸惨剧的头一桩事发生了。当天早班邮件送来父亲一封信，说他有一件极私密的事要见我，约我去他那儿吃饭，当晚就在庄园过夜。他嘱咐我把信销毁，也不要向任何人提起此事。

===Original===
“Considerably surprised, I burnt the note, and duly went out to Luce Manor in time for dinner. When the meal was over my father and I retired to his study, and there when our cigars were alight, he said he had a very grievous and terrible secret to impart to me which would doubtless give me considerable pain. He locked the door, then sitting down he told me what I believe you already know.

===Chinese===
「我暗自称奇，烧掉了信，按时去卢斯庄园赴晚饭。饭后，我和父亲退进他的书房；雪茄点上之后，他说，有一桩沉痛骇人的秘密要向我吐露，必定会叫我十分难过。他锁上门，坐下来，把那件事讲给了我——我相信，这件事你们已经知道了。

===Original===
“ ‘My boy,’ he said, ‘we have not perhaps pulled it off together as well as I could have wished, and when you hear what I have to tell you, I fear you may be tempted to think more bitterly of me than I deserve. But I can assure you on my honour, that in this terrible affair I acted in perfectly good faith all through. Until four years ago I was as ignorant as you are still that there was anything wrong.’

===Chinese===
「『孩子，』他说，『我们爷儿俩也许处得不如我希望的那样好；等你听完我要讲的话，我怕你会忍不住把我看得比实情更坏。但我可以以名誉向你担保：在这件可怕的事情里，我自始至终问心无愧。直到四年前，我还和你现在一样，压根儿不知道有什么不对。』」

===Original===
“ ‘I don’t understand,’ I said.

===Chinese===
「『我不明白，』我说。

===Original===
“ ‘No,’ he answered, ‘but you will soon.’

===Chinese===
「『是的，』他答道，『可你很快就会明白。』」

===Original===
“Then he told me of his early life, and that of the two Dales; of his falling in love with my mother, Ethel Osborne; of the rivalry between himself and Tom Dale for her hand; of Dale’s success; of the miserable married life of the couple; of Dale’s mission to Canada, and of his presumed death in the *Numidian* disaster, and of my father’s own marriage with the widow. All this I had known more or less vaguely before, and I could not understand why my father recited the circumstances in such detail. But he soon made it clear to me.

===Chinese===
「接着他讲起自己的早年，讲起戴尔兄弟俩；讲起他怎样爱上我母亲埃塞尔·奥斯本；讲起他与汤姆·戴尔如何竞逐她的芳心；戴尔如何如愿以偿；那两口子婚后的日子怎样凄惶；戴尔奉差去加拿大，在『努米底亚人』号海难中推定罹难；以及我父亲后来怎样娶了这位寡居的母亲。这些事我以前多多少少隐约知道，不明白父亲为什么要把情节叙得这样细。但他很快让我明白了。

===Original===
“ ‘As you know,’ he went on, ‘your dear mother and I have lived happily together ever since. She had her time of suffering, but thank God, she has enjoyed her afterlife, and please God, she shall never learn what I am about to tell you.’

===Chinese===
「『你知道，』他接下去说，『自那以后，你亲爱的母亲和我一直过得和美。她受过她的苦，但感谢上帝，她后半生享了福；还求上帝保佑，她永远别知道我如今要告诉你的事。』」

===Original===
“ ‘Some four years ago,’ continued my father, ‘I happened to be in London, and walking down Cheapside I met a man whose face seemed vaguely familiar. He was short and slight, with small features, rather delicately moulded, white hair, and a short goatee beard. He saw me at the same time, and his eyes fixed themselves on my face with an expression of almost incredulous recognition. For a few seconds we stood facing each other, while I racked my brains to recall his identity. And then suddenly I knew him. It was Tom Dale!’

===Chinese===
「『大约四年前，』父亲继续说，『我恰好在伦敦。那天我正沿奇普赛德（Cheapside）街走，迎面撞见一个人，那张脸隐隐有些眼熟。他个子矮小瘦弱，五官细巧秀气，头发花白，蓄着一撮山羊短须。他也同时瞧见了我，两眼死死盯住我的脸，那神情几乎是不敢相认。我们对面立了几秒钟，我苦苦地搜索记忆，想认出他是谁。突然，我想起来了。是汤姆·戴尔！』」

===Original===
“My father paused, but for some seconds I did not grasp the full meaning of his statement. Then gradually its significance dawned on me. I need not repeat it. You have heard what it involved. I was appalled and horrified. Though upset on my own account, I ask you to believe that what distressed me most was its possible effect on my mother and sister. Of my mother I just couldn’t bear to think, and it also hurt me beyond words to believe that any such secret should have power to throw a shadow over Enid’s life.”

===Chinese===
「父亲住了口。有几秒钟，我没有领会他这句话的全部分量。渐渐地，它的含义才透进我心里。含义我不必重复，它牵连着什么，你们已经听过。我惊得目瞪口呆，又怕得魂飞魄散。固然我自己也乱了方寸，但我请你们相信：最叫我痛苦的，是想到它可能落到母亲和妹妹身上。母亲那边，我简直不敢往下想；一想到这样一个秘密竟有力量在伊妮德的人生上投下阴影，我也痛心得无以言表。」

===Original===
“Did you speak to your father on this particular point?” Tanner interjected.

===Chinese===
「这一点您跟父亲说了吗？」坦纳插话道。

===Original===
“Speak? I should rather think so. I was beside myself with horror.”

===Chinese===
「说？岂止是说了。我当时怕得魂儿都没了。」

===Original===
“Can you recollect the exact words you used?”

===Chinese===
「您还记得自己当时用的原话吗？」

===Original===
Austin considered.

===Chinese===
奥斯汀想了想。

===Original===
“I hardly think so,” he said at last, “though every detail of the scene is fixed in my memory; I said as the thing began to dawn on me, ‘And my mother﻿—it can’t be that she﻿—?’ I did not wish to speak the words, and my father completed my sentence for me. ‘Yes,’ he said, ‘there’s no escape from it; she is the wife of that drunken ruffian.’ Then I cried, ‘Good Heavens! She can’t be,’ or something to that effect, and he answered that it was only too true.”

===Chinese===
「怕是记不真了，」他终于说，「不过那一幕的每个细节都钉在我记忆里。事情刚在我脑子里露出眉目时，我说：『那我母亲——总不会是她——？』我不愿把话挑明，父亲替我把后半句补上了。『是的，』他说，『躲不掉的；她就是那个醉鬼恶棍的妻子。』于是我嚷道：『天哪！不可能的，』或者类似的话；他回答说，不幸这正是事实。」

===Original===
“Might the words you used have been, ‘My God, sir, she isn’t?’ ”

===Chinese===
「您当时的话，会不会是这么几个字：『我的上帝，先生，她不是的吧？』」

===Original===
“Yes, I believe those were the words. That was the sense anyway.”

===Chinese===
「对，我想就是这几个字。反正意思就是这个意思。」

===Original===
“Continue, please.”

===Chinese===
「请讲下去。」

===Original===
“It appeared that upon their recognition there was a scene between my father and Dale. Eventually, however, they took a private room at a neighbouring bar, and there talked the matter over. Then to my father’s amazement it came out that Dale had not known of my mother’s second marriage. But when the latter realised how matters stood, his manner changed. He said it was my father, and not himself, who had come within the reach of the law, and that if the affair became known, even if my father escaped imprisonment, he would still have public opinion to reckon with. Was he prepared to face the scandal?

===Chinese===
「原来，两人相认之后，父亲和戴尔之间闹了一场。不过末了，他们在近旁一家酒馆要了个单间，把事情谈开了。随后，令父亲大吃一惊的是，戴尔竟不知道我母亲改嫁的事。可等他弄清了局势，态度立刻变了。他说：触犯法律的是我父亲，不是他本人；这件事一旦传扬出去，就算我父亲逃得过牢狱之灾，也还得同舆论算账。他还问：这桩丑闻，可有胆量面对？

===Original===
“My father was not. His good name and that of his family were very precious to him. In his agitation he did a weak thing. He offered to buy Dale’s silence.

===Chinese===
「父亲没有这个胆量。他自己的名誉和全家的名誉，于他贵重无比。慌乱中他做了一件软弱的事：他提出出钱收买戴尔的缄默。

===Original===
“My father then told of his negotiations with Dale, with the details of which I think I need hardly trouble you. Suffice it to say that Dale put on the screw and got several hundreds that year. But that did not satisfy him. His demands grew more and more outrageous till at last my father came to the conclusion that some step must be taken to rid himself of the incubus.

===Chinese===
「父亲接着讲了他同戴尔交涉的经过，其中细节我想不必烦扰诸位。只消说一句：戴尔拧紧了螺丝，那一年榨去了几百镑。但这填不满他。他的索求越抬越离谱，到最后父亲断定，必须设法甩脱这个附骨之疽。

===Original===
“ ‘It is not the actual money he is now getting,’ said my father, ‘it is the uncertainty under which I am living that is making me ill. He will continue to bleed me, and after I am gone he will bleed your mother and yourself, and perhaps Enid. Besides, we don’t know if he really will preserve the secret. I have been thinking for some time that I must tell you and Cosgrove the whole story, so that we may devise some plan to protect ourselves, but now events have been precipitated by a fresh demand from Dale. Read that.’

===Chinese===
「『现在叫他刮走的这点钱还不是要害，』父亲说，『要害是我这么提心吊胆地活着，人都熬病了。他还要继续放我的血；等我死了，还要放你母亲和你的血，说不定还有伊妮德的。再说，咱们也拿不准他到底守不守得住秘密。我早就在想，必须把整件事原原本本告诉你和科斯格罗夫，好合计出个法子保全自己；如今戴尔又来了新的索求，事情提前发作了。你自己看吧。』」

===Original===
“My father handed me a letter headed ‘Myrtle Cottage, Yelverton, South Devon.’ It was from Dale, and in it he said the existing arrangements with my father were unsatisfactory, that instead of a hundred or two now and then, he would rather have one large sum which should close the account between them. He demanded my father should buy him an annuity which should bring him in £500 a year for life. In peremptory words he required an immediate answer, adding that he was coming to town that day and would stay in a small hotel near Gower Street, where my father could see him.

===Chinese===
「父亲递给我一封信，信头是『德文郡南耶尔弗顿，桃金娘小屋』。信是戴尔写的。他在信里说，目前与我父亲的安排不能令人满意；与其零零星星、时不时给个一百两百，他宁可一次拿一大笔，把两人间的账了结干净。他要求我父亲给他买一份年金，每年 500 镑，付他终身。他以强硬的字眼勒令立即答复，并说他当天就进城，住在高尔街附近一家小旅馆里，我父亲可以到那儿见他。

===Original===
“That letter had come on the previous Thursday, and on the Saturday﻿—that was the day before our interview﻿—my father had gone to town and seen Dale. The man, it seemed, had been more truculent and overbearing than ever before, and had presented what amounted to an ultimatum. Either he would have the money for his annuity, or he would tell. After a long wrangle my father had promised to consider the matter until the following Monday, when he would see Dale again and let him know his decision.

===Chinese===
「那封信是前一个星期四到的。星期六——就是我们父子面谈的前一天——父亲进了城，见了戴尔。据说那人比以往哪一回都蛮横倨傲，端出了一份不折不扣的最后通牒：要么给钱买年金，要么他就抖出去。长吵一场之后，父亲答应考虑到下一个星期一，届时再见戴尔，把决定告诉他。

===Original===
“My father went on to say he would willingly pay the demand to be rid of the whole business, but his difficulty was, of course, that he had no guarantee the payment would rid him of it. He would still be, to precisely the same extent, in Dale’s power.

===Chinese===
「父亲接着说，他情愿照付，好把整件事了断干净；难处自然在于：付了钱，并无保证真能脱身。他仍旧会分毫不差地捏在戴尔手心里。

===Original===
“He continued that he felt that as Cosgrove and I were also interested in the affair, the time had now come to take us into his confidence, in order to see if some joint action could not be taken to bring the matter to an end. He had not yet spoken to Cosgrove, but he suggested that on the following day, Monday, we should both go to town and have an interview with my cousin, after which he could go on and see Dale. We decided to travel separately, to meet at a little French restaurant in Soho, and to keep the matter perfectly private.”

===Chinese===
「他继续说，他觉得既然科斯格罗夫和我也利益攸关，如今该让我们知情了，看看能否同心协力，把这件事做个了断。他还没有跟科斯格罗夫开过口，但他提议，第二天、也就是星期一，我们父子一起进城，先同我表哥谈，之后他再接着去见戴尔。我们决定分头前往，在索霍区一家小法国餐馆碰头，事情绝对保密。」

===Original===
Austin once again paused and glanced round the little group. He was speaking quietly, but there was a ring of truth in his voice. Tanner, who sat checking his every statement in the light of what he had himself learnt, and watching like a lynx for discrepancies, had to admit to himself that the story they were hearing was consistent with the facts.
